\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}U(t)\big|_{\infty}\leq C(a,L,T)\big(1+\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U(t)\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U(t)\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$},

where the cut-off function
\zeta^{\sharp}_{a}
is defined in
§
3.1.3
.

Proof.

It follows from Lemmas
6.1
and
A.3
that

\displaystyle|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}U|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle=\big|(\zeta_{a}^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2L}U^{2}\big|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}\big|((\zeta_{a}^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2L}U^{2})_{r}\big|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C(L)\big(\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}(\zeta_{a}^{\sharp})_{r}\rho_{0}^{2L}U^{2}\big|_{1}+\big|(\zeta_{a}^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2L-\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}U^{2}\big|_{1}+\big|(\zeta_{a}^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2L}\eta_{r}UD_{\eta}U\big|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,L)\big(|\chi_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}U|_{2}^{2}+|(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}|_{\infty}\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L-\frac{\beta}{2}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,L,T)\big(1+\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}\big|\zeta_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\big).

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 7.2
.

For any
L>0
, there exists a constant
C(L,T)>0
such that

|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}\eta_{r}(t)|_{1}\leq C(L,T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$},

where
\chi^{\sharp}
denotes the characteristic function on
(\frac{1}{2},1]

(
see
§
3.1.3
)
.

Proof.

Let
L>0
. From the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
, the formula of flow map
\eta
, Lemma
6.1
, and integration by parts, we obtain

\displaystyle|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L}\eta_{r}|_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{1}\rho_{0}^{L}\eta_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{1}\rho_{0}^{L}\,\mathrm{d}\eta=\underline{-\rho_{0}^{L}\big(\frac{1}{2}\big)\eta\big(t,\frac{1}{2}\big)}_{\,\leq 0}-\frac{L}{\beta}\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{1}\rho_{0}^{L-\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}\eta\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C(L)|(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}|_{\infty}\Big(|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L-\beta}r|_{1}+\int_{0}^{t}|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{L-\beta}U|_{1}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\leq C(L,T).

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 7.3
.

Let
q_{*}
be a parameter such that

(7.1)

q_{*}:=\begin{cases}\displaystyle(2-\gamma)^{-1}&\displaystyle\text{if $1<\gamma<\frac{3}{2}$},\\
2&\displaystyle\text{if $\gamma\geq\frac{3}{2}$}.\end{cases}

Then, for any
N\geq 3\beta
, there exists a constant
C(N,T)>0
such that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(7.2)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(N,T)\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2},

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{N+\beta}V_{r}|_{q_{*}}

\displaystyle\leq C(N,T)\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C(N,T)\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}},

where the cut-off function
\zeta^{\sharp}
is defined in
§
3.1.3
.

Proof.

Let
q_{*}
be defined as (
7.1
) and
N\geq 3\beta
. We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
Multiplying (
4.2
) by
2(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2N}\eta_{r}V
gives

((\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2N}\eta_{r}V^{2})_{t}+\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2N}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\eta_{r}V(V-U)=(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2N}V^{2}U_{r}.

Then, integrating the above over
I
, we obtain from (
2.1
), Lemmas
4.13
,
5.1
, and
6.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\varrho^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}^{2}=\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2N}\eta_{r}V\big(\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\varrho^{\gamma-1}U+VD_{\eta}U\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}+|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}V|_{\infty}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(N,T)\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2},

which, along with the fact that
N>N-\beta\geq\frac{N+\beta}{2}
, leads to

(7.3)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}\leq C(N,T)\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}.

2.
Applying
\partial_{r}
to both sides of (
4.2
), together with (
4.1
), gives

V_{tr}+\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V_{r}-U_{r})+\frac{A\gamma(\gamma-1)}{4\mu^{2}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\eta_{r}(V-U)^{2}=0.

Then multiplying the above by

(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2q_{*}}\rho_{0}^{(N+\beta)q_{*}}|V_{r}|^{q_{*}-1}\mathrm{sgn}\,(V_{r})

and integrating the resulting equality over
I
, we obtain from Lemma
5.1
and the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{q_{*}}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{N+\beta}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}^{q_{*}}+\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{N+\beta}\varrho^{\frac{\gamma-1}{q_{*}}}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}^{q_{*}}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2q_{*}}\rho_{0}^{(N+\beta)q_{*}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\Big(\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}D_{\eta}U-\frac{A\gamma(\gamma-1)}{4\mu^{2}}(V-U)^{2}\Big)\eta_{r}|V_{r}|^{q_{*}-1}\mathrm{sgn}\,(V_{r})\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\frac{r^{m}}{\eta^{m}}\Big|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2q_{*}}\rho_{0}^{(N+\beta)q_{*}+\gamma-1}\eta_{r}^{2-\gamma}(|D_{\eta}U|+|V|^{2}+|U|^{2})|V_{r}|^{q_{*}-1}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{N+\beta+\gamma-1}\eta_{r}^{2-\gamma}(D_{\eta}U,V^{2},U^{2})\big|_{q_{*}}\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{N+\beta}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}^{q_{*}-1},

which implies

(7.4)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{N+\beta}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}\leq C(T)\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{N+\beta+\gamma-1}\eta_{r}^{2-\gamma}(D_{\eta}U,V^{2},U^{2})\big|_{q_{*}}:=\mathrm{R}_{q_{*}}.

Next, for
\mathrm{R}_{q_{*}}
, by
N-\beta\geq\frac{N+\beta}{2}
, Lemmas
6.3
and
7.1
, and the Hölder inequality, we have

(7.5)

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{q_{*}}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2\beta}}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}+|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}V|_{\infty}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}U\big|_{\infty}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}\big)\underline{\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\eta_{r}^{\frac{3}{2}-\gamma}\big|_{\frac{2q_{*}}{2-q_{*}}}}_{:=\widetilde{\mathrm{R}}_{q_{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(N,T)\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\widetilde{\mathrm{R}}_{q_{*}},

where, for
\widetilde{\mathrm{R}}_{q_{*}}
, we can use the definition of
q_{*}
in (
7.1
), and Lemmas
5.1
and
7.2
to derive

(7.6)

\displaystyle\text{if $\gamma\geq\frac{3}{2}$:}

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathrm{R}}_{q_{*}}=\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\eta_{r}^{\frac{3}{2}-\gamma}\big|_{\infty}\leq C(T);

\displaystyle\text{if $1<\gamma<\frac{3}{2}$:}

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathrm{R}}_{q_{*}}=\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\eta_{r}^{\frac{3}{2}-\gamma}\big|_{\frac{2}{3-2\gamma}}=\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{2\gamma-2}{3-2\gamma}}\eta_{r}\big|_{1}^{\frac{3}{2}-\gamma}\leq C(T).

Therefore, collecting (
7.4
)–(
7.6
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{N+\beta}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}

\displaystyle\leq C(N,T)\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{N}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C(N,T)\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{N+\beta}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Finally, this, combined with (
7.3
), yields the desired result.
∎

7.1.2.
Uniform upper bounds of
(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})
in the exterior domain

With the help of Lemmas
7.1
–
7.3
, we can first derive
new
(\rho_{0},\eta_{r})
-weighted estimates for
U
.

Lemma 7.4
.

Let
q_{*}
be defined in (
7.1
) of
Lemma
7.3
. Then, for any
M\geq 2\beta
, there exists a constant
C(M,T)>0
such that

\mathcal{E}_{M}^{\sharp}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}U|_{\infty}^{4}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(M,T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$},

where
\mathcal{E}_{M}^{\sharp}(t)
is defined by

(7.7)

\mathcal{E}_{M}^{\sharp}(t):=\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2M-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}V_{r}(t)\big|_{q_{*}}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U(t)\big|_{2}^{2}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
We first let
M
satisfy

(7.8)

2\beta\leq M\leq\beta+\gamma-1.

Then multiplying
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{1}
by
2(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\eta_{r}\varrho^{-1}\rho_{0}^{2M}U
, along with (
2.1
) and (
4.1
), yields

(7.9)

\displaystyle((\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\eta_{r}U^{2})_{t}\underline{-4\mu(\varrho D_{\eta}U)_{r}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\frac{\rho_{0}^{2M}}{\varrho}U}_{:=\mathrm{L}_{1}}\ \underline{-4\mu m(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)_{r}U}_{:=\mathrm{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle=(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}U^{2}U_{r}-\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\eta_{r}(V-U)U.

Here, we continue to calculate
\mathrm{L}_{1}
and
\mathrm{L}_{2}
. For
\mathrm{L}_{1}
, thanks to (
4.1
), we obtain

(7.10)

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{1}

\displaystyle=4\mu\varrho D_{\eta}U\Big((\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\frac{\rho_{0}^{2M}}{\varrho}U\Big)_{r}-4\mu((\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}UD_{\eta}U)_{r}

\displaystyle=4\mu(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\eta_{r}|D_{\eta}U|^{2}+\frac{8\mu M}{\beta}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M-\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}UD_{\eta}U

\displaystyle-2(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}(V-U)UU_{r}+8\mu\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\rho_{0}^{2M}UD_{\eta}U-4\mu((\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}UD_{\eta}U)_{r}.

Furthermore,
\mathrm{L}_{2}
can be further simplified by

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{2}

\displaystyle=-4\mu m(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\frac{UU_{r}}{\eta}+4\mu m(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\frac{\eta_{r}U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}

\displaystyle=-2\mu m(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\frac{(U^{2})_{r}}{\eta}+4\mu m(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\frac{\eta_{r}U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}

\displaystyle=2\mu m(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\frac{\eta_{r}U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}+4\mu m\Big(\frac{M}{\beta}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\beta}}+\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\Big)\rho_{0}^{2M}\frac{U^{2}}{\eta}-2\mu m\Big((\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\frac{U^{2}}{\eta}\Big)_{r}.

Consequently, this, combined with (
7.9
)–(
7.10
), gives

\displaystyle((\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\eta_{r}U^{2})_{t}+2\mu(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\eta_{r}\Big(2|D_{\eta}U|^{2}+m\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}(2V-U)UU_{r}-\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\eta_{r}(V-U)U

\displaystyle-4\mu\Big(\frac{M}{\beta}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\beta}}\!+\!\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\Big)\rho_{0}^{2M}U\Big(2D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\Big)\!+\!\Big(2\mu(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}U\big(2D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big)_{r}.

Integrating the resulting equality over
I
, we can eventually arrive at

(7.11)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+2\mu\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}(2V-U)UU_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r-\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\eta_{r}(V-U)U\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-4\mu\int_{0}^{1}\Big(\frac{M}{\beta}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\beta}}+\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\Big)\rho_{0}^{2M}U\Big(2D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=15}^{17}\mathrm{G}_{i}.

Now, let
q_{*}
be defined in (
7.1
). For
\mathrm{G}_{15}
, it follows from the facts that

M-\beta>-\frac{\beta}{2},\qquad\frac{2M-\beta}{3}>-\frac{\beta}{3},

integration by parts, Lemmas
6.1
and
6.3
, and the Hölder inequality that

(7.12)

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{15}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}V(U^{2})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r-\frac{1}{3}\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}(U^{3})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}V_{r}U^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r+2\int_{0}^{1}\Big(\frac{M}{\beta}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\beta}}+\zeta^{\sharp}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\Big)\rho_{0}^{2M}\big(\frac{1}{3}U-V\big)U^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}|\chi^{\sharp}U|_{\frac{2q_{*}}{q_{*}-1}}^{2}+C(M)|((\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r},\rho_{0}^{\beta})|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{2M-\beta}{3}}U\big|_{3}^{3}

\displaystyle+C(M)|((\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r},\rho_{0}^{\beta})|_{\infty}|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}V|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M-\beta}U\big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(M,T)\big(\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}+1\big).

For
\mathrm{G}_{16}
, it follows from (
2.1
), the fact that

M+\gamma-1\geq 2M-\beta,

Lemmas
4.13
and
5.1
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities, we have

(7.13)

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{16}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\frac{r^{m}}{\eta^{m}\eta_{r}}\Big|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M+\gamma-1}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}+C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(M,T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2M-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}\big).

For
\mathrm{G}_{17}
, it follows from Lemma
6.1
, the fact that

M-\beta>-\frac{\beta}{2},

and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.14)

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{17}

\displaystyle\leq C(M)|\eta_{r}^{-\frac{1}{2}}|_{\infty}(|(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}|_{\infty}+|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty})\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M-\beta}U\big|_{2}\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(M,T)+\frac{\mu}{8}\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Collecting (
7.11
)–(
7.14
) yields

(7.15)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+\mu\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(M,T)\big(1+\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2M-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}\big).

2. Closing estimates.
Now, setting

N=2M-\beta\ \quad\,\,(N\geq 3\beta\ \ \text{since}\,M\geq 2\beta)

in (
7.2
) of Lemma
7.3
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2M-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(M,T)\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2},

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}V_{r}|_{q_{*}}

\displaystyle\leq C(M,T)\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2M-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C(M,T)\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}},

which, combined with (
7.15
) and the Young inequality, gives that, for all
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\varepsilon\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2M-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}^{2}+|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{2M}V_{r}|_{q_{*}}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}\big)+\mu\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(M,T)\varepsilon\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{2}+C(M,T)\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2M-\beta}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V_{r}\big|_{q_{*}}\big)

\displaystyle+C(\varepsilon,M,T)\big(1+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}\big).

Hence, setting

\varepsilon=\min\big\{\frac{\mu}{10C(M,T)},\frac{1}{2}\big\},

and then applying the Grönwall inequality to the resulting inequality yield that, for all
M
satisfying (
7.8
),

(7.16)

\mathcal{E}_{M}^{\sharp}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(M,T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$},

where
\mathcal{E}^{\sharp}_{M}
is defined in (
7.7
).

Moreover, it follows from Lemma
7.1
and (
7.16
) that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}U|_{\infty}^{4}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(M,T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(M,T)\Big(1+\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\mathcal{E}_{M}^{\sharp}(t)\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{M}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\leq C(M,T).

3.
Finally, for
M>\beta+\gamma-1
, we can simply use the fact that
\rho_{0}\in L^{\infty}
to obtain the desired estimates. This completes the proof of Lemma
7.4
.
∎

Now, we can derive the uniform upper bounds of
(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})
in
[0,T]\times[\frac{5}{8},1]
.

Lemma 7.5
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}^{\sharp}\frac{\eta(t,r)}{r}+\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}^{\sharp}\eta_{r}(t,r)\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
We can first obtain from
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La}_{1}
, the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
, Lemma
4.1
, and the Hölder inequality that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(7.17)

\displaystyle\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\rho_{0}\big|_{2}+C_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}+C_{0}\sqrt{t}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C(T).

2. Weighted
L^{\infty}
-estimate of
\log\varrho
in the exterior domain.
Consider the quantity

(7.18)

\rho_{0}^{K}\log\varrho=\rho_{0}^{K}\log\big(\frac{r^{m}\rho_{0}}{\eta^{m}\eta_{r}}\big)

with a fixed number

(7.19)

K:=100(\beta+\gamma).

2.1.
First, we obtain from (
4.1
), (
7.17
), Lemmas
7.4
and
A.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(7.20)

\displaystyle\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\log\varrho\big|_{\infty}^{2}\leq C_{0}\big\|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K}\log^{2}\varrho\big\|_{1,1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{K}\log\varrho\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K-\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}\log^{2}\varrho\big|_{1}+\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K}\eta_{r}\log\varrho(V,U)\big|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|(\rho_{0}^{\beta},(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r})|_{\infty}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{K-\frac{\beta}{2}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(V,U)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}+1\big).

2.2.
Multiplying
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La}_{1}
by
2(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\eta_{r}\rho_{0}^{2K}\varrho^{-1}\log\varrho
, along with (
4.1
), gives

\displaystyle\big((\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K}\eta_{r}\log^{2}\varrho\big)_{t}+2(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K}\eta_{r}(\log\varrho)\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)

\displaystyle=\big((\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K}(\log^{2}\varrho)U\big)_{r}-4(\zeta^{\sharp})^{3}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\rho_{0}^{2K}(\log^{2}\varrho)U

\displaystyle-\frac{2K}{\beta}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K-\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}(\log^{2}\varrho)U-\frac{1}{\mu}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K}\eta_{r}\log\varrho(V-U)U.

Then, integrating the above equality over
I
, we have

(7.21)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=-2\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K}\eta_{r}(\log\varrho)\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r-\frac{1}{\mu}\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}\rho_{0}^{2K}\eta_{r}\log\varrho(V-U)U\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{1}\Big(\frac{2K}{\beta}(\zeta^{\sharp})^{4}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}+4(\zeta^{\sharp})^{3}(\zeta^{\sharp})_{r}\rho_{0}^{\beta}\Big)\rho_{0}^{2K-\beta}(\log^{2}\varrho)U\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=18}^{20}\mathrm{G}_{i}.

For
\mathrm{G}_{18}
–
\mathrm{G}_{20}
, it follows from (
7.17
), (
7.20
), Lemma
7.4
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.22)

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{18}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}^{2}+C_{0}\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{19}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\log\varrho\big|_{\infty}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{K}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(V,U)\big|_{2}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{K}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}+1\big)\leq\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}^{2}+C(T),

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{20}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(\rho_{0}^{\beta},(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r})|_{\infty}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\varrho\big|_{2}^{2}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2K-\beta-1}U\big|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\big(\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2K-\beta-1}U\big|_{\infty}^{4}+1\big).

Combining (
7.21
)–(
7.22
), along with the Grönwall inequality and Lemma
7.4
, implies that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho(t)\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\log\rho_{0}\big|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{2K-\beta-1}U\big|_{\infty}^{4}\,\mathrm{d}s+C(T)\leq C(T).

Finally, this, together with (
7.20
), yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(7.23)

\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\log\varrho(t)\big|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\big(\big|(\zeta^{\sharp})^{2}\rho_{0}^{K}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho(t)\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}+1\big)\leq C(T).

3. Uniform upper bounds of
(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})
in
[0,T]\times[\frac{5}{8},1]
.
Let
K
be defined in (
7.19
). First, (
7.23
), along with (
7.18
) and Lemma
5.1
, implies that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times[\frac{5}{8},1]
,

(7.24)

\eta_{r}(t,r)\leq\frac{r^{m}\rho_{0}(r)}{\eta^{m}(t,r)}\exp(C(T)\rho_{0}^{-K}(r))\leq C(T)\rho_{0}(r)\exp(C(T)\rho_{0}^{-K}(r)).

Now we show that, for all
T>0
, there exists a finite constant
C(T)>1
depending only on
(C_{0},T)
such that

(7.25)

\eta_{r}(t,r)\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\big[\frac{5}{8},1\big]$}.

Indeed, assume contrarily that there exist a time
T>0
and a sequence of
\{(t_{k},r_{k})\}_{k=1}^{\infty}\subset[0,T]\times[\frac{5}{8},1]
so that

(7.26)

\eta_{r}(t_{k},r_{k})\to\infty\qquad\text{as $k\to\infty$}.

Note that, for any fixed constants
q_{0},q_{1}>0
and
s\in[0,q_{1}]
,

s\exp(q_{0}s^{-K})\to\infty\qquad\text{whenever $s\to 0$}.

Consequently, it follows from the above, the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
, (
7.24
), and (
7.26
) that

(7.27)

C(T)\rho_{0}(r_{k})\exp(C(T)\rho_{0}^{-K}(r_{k}))\geq\eta_{r}(t_{k},r_{k})\to\infty\qquad\text{as $k\to\infty$},

which implies

r_{k}\to 1\qquad\text{as $k\to\infty$}.

However, this contradicts to the fact that
\eta_{r}|_{r=1}=1
for each
t\in[0,T]
due to
U_{r}|_{r=1}=0
. Hence (
7.25
) holds.

Finally, the upper bound of
\frac{\eta}{r}
in
[0,T]\times[\frac{5}{8},1]
follows from (
7.25
) and Lemmas
6.1
and
A.3
:

\displaystyle\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}^{\sharp}\frac{\eta(t,r)}{r}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8}{5}\|\eta(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\frac{5}{8},1)}\leq C_{0}\int_{\frac{5}{8}}^{1}\eta(t,r)\,\mathrm{d}r+C_{0}\int_{\frac{5}{8}}^{1}\eta_{r}(t,r)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\int_{\frac{5}{8}}^{1}\Big(r+\int_{0}^{t}U(s,r)\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r+C(T)\leq C_{0}\int_{0}^{t}|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}^{\sharp}U|_{1}\,\mathrm{d}s+C(T)\leq C(T).

This completes the proof of Lemma
7.5
.
∎

7.2.
Uniform upper bounds of
(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})
near the symmetric center

Lemma 7.6
.

For any
a\in(0,1)
, there exists a constant
C(a,T)
such that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|\zeta_{a}U|_{\infty}^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(a,T).

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
First, multiplying
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{1}
by
2\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}\varrho^{-1}U
with
a\in(0,1)
, along with (
4.1
), gives

\displaystyle(\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}U^{2})_{t}+2\mu\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}\Big(2|D_{\eta}U|^{2}+m\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\Big(4\mu\zeta_{a}^{2}UD_{\eta}U+2\mu m\zeta_{a}^{2}\frac{U^{2}}{\eta}-\zeta_{a}^{2}U^{3}\Big)_{r}-\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V-U)U+2\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}VUD_{\eta}U

\displaystyle-2\zeta_{a}(\zeta_{a})_{r}\Big(4\mu UD_{\eta}U+2\mu m\frac{U^{2}}{\eta}-U^{3}\Big).

Integrating the above over
I
leads to

(7.28)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U|_{2}^{2}+\mu\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\int_{0}^{1}\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V-U)U\,\mathrm{d}r+2\int_{0}^{1}\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}VUD_{\eta}U\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{1}\zeta_{a}(\zeta_{a})_{r}\Big(4\mu UD_{\eta}U+2\mu m\frac{U^{2}}{\eta}-U^{3}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=18}^{20}\mathrm{G}_{i}.

For
\mathrm{G}_{18}
–
\mathrm{G}_{20}
, from Lemmas
4.6
,
4.13
,
5.1
, and
6.6
, the fact that
\support(\zeta_{a})_{r}\subset[a,\frac{1+3a}{4}]
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities, it follows that

(7.29)

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{18}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U|_{2}\big(\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}V\big|_{2}+|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U|_{2}\big)\leq C(T)\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,\varrho^{\gamma-1}V)\big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{19}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}V\big|_{\infty}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U|_{2}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U|_{2}\leq C(a,T)|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{8}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathrm{G}_{20}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U\big|_{2}\Big(\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{4}}U\big|_{4}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)+\frac{\mu}{8}\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Therefore, we obtain from (
7.28
)–(
7.29
) that

(7.30)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{2}\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(a,T)\big(\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,\varrho^{\gamma-1}V)\big|_{2}^{2}+1\big).

2.
Multiplying (
4.2
) by
2\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}\varrho^{2\gamma-2}V
, together with
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La}_{1}
, we have

(\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}\varrho^{2\gamma-2}V^{2})_{t}=\zeta_{a}^{2}\varrho^{2\gamma-2}V^{2}\eta_{r}\big((3-2\gamma)D_{\eta}U-(2\gamma-2)m\frac{U}{\eta}\big)-\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\zeta_{a}^{2}\eta_{r}\varrho^{3\gamma-3}V(V-U).

Then, integrating the resulting equality over
I
, we can obtain from Lemmas
4.13
and
6.6
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.31)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}V\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}V\big|_{\infty}\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}V\big|_{2}\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\big(\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}V\big|_{2}+|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}\big)|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}V\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,\varrho^{\gamma-1}V)\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{8}\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

3.
Therefore, combining (
7.30
)–(
7.31
) leads to

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(U,\varrho^{\gamma-1}V\big)\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{8}\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(a,T)\big(\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,\varrho^{\gamma-1}V)\big|_{2}^{2}+1\big),

which, along with the Grönwall inequality, yields that, for all
a\in(0,1)
and
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(U,\varrho^{\gamma-1}V)(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big(\big|\zeta_{a}(u_{0},\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}v_{0})\big|_{2}^{2}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T).

Here, to derive the
L^{2}
-bound of
\zeta_{a}(u_{0},\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}v_{0})
, it suffices to check that

|\zeta_{a}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}(\log\rho_{0})_{r}|_{2}\leq|\zeta_{a}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1-\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}|_{2}\leq C(a)|(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}|_{\infty}\leq C(a).

Finally, it follows from the above, Lemmas
5.1
and
A.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that, for all
a\in(0,1)
and
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|\zeta_{a}U|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\big|\zeta_{a}U((\zeta_{a})_{r}U,\zeta_{a}U_{r})\big|_{1}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(a)\int_{0}^{t}\big(\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U\big|_{2}^{2}+|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U|_{2}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U|_{2}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)+\int_{0}^{t}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s.

This completes the proof of Lemma
7.6
.
∎

We now establish the global uniform upper bounds of
(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})
in
[0,T]\times[0,a]
for
a\in(0,1)
.

Lemma 7.7
.

For any
a\in(0,1)
, there exists a constant
C(a,T)>0
such that

\chi_{a}\frac{\eta(t,r)}{r}+\chi_{a}\eta_{r}(t,r)\leq C(a,T)\qquad\text{for all $(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}$},

where
\chi_{a}
denotes the characteristic function on
[0,a]

(
see
§
3.1.3
)
.

Proof.

We divide the proof of this lemma into three steps.

1.
First, multiplying (
4.2
) by
2\chi_{a}\eta_{r}V
(
a\in(0,1)
) and integrating the resulting equality over
I
, together with Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
, and
7.6
, and the Hölder inequality, give

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V|_{2}^{2}+\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\big|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\varrho^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}V\big|_{2}^{2}=\int_{0}^{a}V^{2}U_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r+\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\int_{0}^{a}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-1}UV\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq|\zeta_{a}V|_{\infty}|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V|_{2}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U|_{2}+\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V\big|_{2}|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}U|_{2},

which, along with the Young inequality, leads to

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V|_{2}^{2}\leq C(a,T)\big(|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V|_{2}^{2}+|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}U|_{2}^{2}+1\big).

Then applying the Grönwall inequality to the above and using Lemma
7.6
, we obtain that, for any
t\in[0,T]
and
a\in(0,1)
,

(7.32)

|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}V(t)|_{2}\leq C(a,T)(|\chi_{a}v_{0}|_{2}+1)\leq C(a,T).

Here, to derive the
L^{2}
-bound of
\chi_{a}v_{0}
, it suffices to note that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
and

|\chi_{a}(\log\rho_{0})_{r}|_{2}\leq C(a)|(\rho_{0})_{r}|_{2}\leq C(a).

2. Uniform boundedness of
\log\varrho
near the origin.
First, it follows from (
4.1
), (
7.32
), Lemmas
5.1
,
7.6
, and
A.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.33)

\displaystyle|\chi_{a}\log\varrho|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a)|\chi_{a}\log\varrho|_{2}^{2}+C(a)|\chi_{a}(\log\varrho)(\log\varrho)_{r}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C(a)|\chi_{a}\log\varrho|_{2}^{2}+C(a)|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho|_{2}|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}D_{\eta}\log\varrho|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a)(|\eta_{r}^{-1}|_{\infty}+1)|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho|_{2}^{2}+\big|\sqrt{\eta_{r}}(\chi_{a}V,\zeta_{a}U)\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)(|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho|_{2}^{2}+1).

Next, multiplying
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La}_{1}
by
2\chi_{a}\eta_{r}\varrho^{-1}\log\varrho
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
, we obtain from the above, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{a}(\log\varrho)^{2}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}U\,\mathrm{d}r-2\int_{0}^{a}\eta_{r}(\log\varrho)\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}(|\chi_{a}\log\varrho|_{\infty}+1)|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho|_{2}\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}(|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho|_{2}^{2}+1)\Big|\zeta_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2},

which, along with Lemma
7.6
, the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
, and the Grönwall and Hölder inequalities, yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
and
a\in(0,1)
,

(7.34)

|\chi_{a}\sqrt{\eta_{r}}\log\varrho(t)|_{2}\leq C(a,T)(|\chi_{a}\log\rho_{0}|_{2}+1)\leq C(a,T).

This, together with (
7.33
), also yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
and
a\in(0,1)
,

(7.35)

|\chi_{a}\log\varrho(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(a,T).

3. Upper bounds for
(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})
in
[0,T]\times[0,a]
.
Note that (
7.35
), together with (
2.1
), implies that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times[0,a]
and
a\in(0,1)
,

(7.36)

\frac{r^{m}\rho_{0}(r)}{(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})(t,r)}\geq C(a,T)^{-1}\implies(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})(t,r)\leq C(a,T)r^{m}.

Therefore, it follows from (
7.36
) and Lemma
5.1
that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times[0,a]
,

\frac{\eta(t,r)}{r}\leq\Big(\frac{C(a,T)}{\eta_{r}(t,r)}\Big)^{\frac{1}{m}}\leq C(a,T),\qquad\eta_{r}(t,r)\leq\frac{C(a,T)r^{m}}{\eta^{m}(t,r)}\leq C(a,T).

This completes the proof of Lemma
7.7
.
∎

Consequently, Lemma
7.7
, combined with Lemma
7.5
, yields that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}
,

\frac{\eta(t,r)}{r}+\eta_{r}(t,r)=\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}\big(\frac{\eta(t,r)}{r}+\eta_{r}(t,r)\big)+\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}^{\sharp}\big(\frac{\eta(t,r)}{r}+\eta_{r}(t,r)\big)\leq C(T).

which thus provides the global uniform upper bounds of
(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})
in
[0,T]\times\bar{I}
:

Lemma 7.8
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\frac{\eta(t,r)}{r}+\eta_{r}(t,r)\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}$}.

8.
Non-Formation of Vacuum States Inside the Fluids in Finite Time

The purpose of this section is to show that there is no vacuum formation inside the fluids in finite time.

Lemma 8.1
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>1
such that

C(T)^{-1}\leq\frac{\varrho(t,r)}{\rho_{0}(r)}\leq C(T)\qquad\,\,\text{for all $(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}$}.

In particular, no vacuum state will occur in
[0,T]\times[0,a]
for any
T>0
and
a\in(0,1)
.

Proof.

It follows from (
2.1
) and Lemmas
5.1
and
7.8
that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}
,

\frac{\varrho(t,r)}{\rho_{0}(r)}=\frac{r^{m}}{(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})(t,r)}\in\big[C(T)^{-1},C(T)\big].

Moreover, since
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
, the above statement implies

\varrho(t,r)>0\ \ \text{in $[0,T]\times[0,a]$}\qquad\text{for any $T>0$ and $a\in(0,1).$}

This completes the proof.
∎

9.
Global-In-Time Uniform Weighted Energy Estimates on the Velocity

The purpose of this section is to establish the global-in-time uniform weighted energy estimates on the velocity. Let
T>0
be a fixed time, and let
(U,\eta)(t,r)
be the unique classical solutions of
IBVP
(
2.2
) in
[0,T]\times\bar{I}
obtained in Theorem
3.1
.
Then density
\varrho
can be given by (
2.1
), and
(\varrho,U,\eta)(t,r)
still solves problem (
1.16
) in
[0,T]\times\bar{I}
. Clearly, this solution is in the class
D(T)
as defined in Definition
4.1
,
and hence the estimates obtained in §
4
–
8
hold for
(U,\eta)
.
Moreover, throughout this section, we always assume that
(\beta,\gamma)
satisfy

\beta\in\big(\frac{1}{3},\gamma-1\big],\qquad\,\gamma\in\big(\frac{4}{3},\infty\big)\ \ \text{if $n=2$},\qquad\ \gamma\in\big(\frac{4}{3},3\big)\ \ \text{if $n=3$}.

Before establishing the uniform estimates for
U
, we first give the following lemma, which can be seen as a variant of the classical div-curl estimates for spherically symmetric functions. This lemma will be frequently used in the later analysis.

Lemma 9.1
.

Let
\bm{f}(\bm{y})=f(r)\frac{\bm{y}}{r}\in C_{\mathrm{c}}^{\infty}(B_{1})
with
B_{1}:=\{\bm{y}:|\bm{y}|<1\}
. For any
a\in(0,1)
, the following estimates hold
:

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}f,\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\sim\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2},

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}f,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\sim\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2},

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{3}f,D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big),\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\sim\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big),\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2},

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{4}f,D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}\big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}(\frac{f}{\eta})\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\sim\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{3}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}\big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta})\big)\Big)\Big|_{2},

where
F_{1}\sim F_{2}
denotes that there exists a constant
C(T)\geq 1
depending only on
(C_{0},T)
such that
C(T)^{-1}F_{1}\leq F_{2}\leq C(T)F_{1}
.

Proof.

For simplicity, we only give the proof for
a=\frac{1}{2}
and show that the quantities on the left-hand sides can be controlled by those on the right-hand sides. Besides, the following fact will be used frequently later:

D_{\eta}\zeta\leq 0\qquad\text{for all $(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}$}.

We divide the proof into four steps.

1.
It follows from integration by parts that

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}f|^{2}+\frac{m^{2}f^{2}}{\eta^{2}}+\frac{2m}{\eta}fD_{\eta}f\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}f|^{2}+\frac{mf^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r-2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta D_{\eta}\zeta\eta^{m-1}\eta_{r}f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\geq\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}f|^{2}+\frac{mf^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r,

which, along with Lemmas
5.1
and
7.8
, leads to

\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}f,\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}.

2.
First, a direct calculation yields

(9.1)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}^{2}f|^{2}+m^{2}\Big|D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r+\underline{2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{2}fD_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=\mathrm{L}_{3}}.

Next, by chain rules, we have

(9.2)

\frac{D_{\eta}^{2}f}{\eta}=D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)+\frac{2}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big).

Then
\mathrm{L}_{3}
can be handled by using (
9.2
) and integration by parts:

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{3}

\displaystyle=4m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r+2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m+1}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=(3m-m^{2})\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r-2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta D_{\eta}\zeta\eta^{m+1}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r\geq 0.

Hence, substituting the above into (
9.1
) leads to

\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}^{2}f|^{2}+m^{2}\Big|D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r,

which, along with Lemmas
5.1
and
7.8
, yields

\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}f,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}.

3.
First, we have

(9.3)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}^{3}f|^{2}+m^{2}\Big|D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r+\underline{2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{3}fD_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=\mathrm{L}_{4}},

and we can obtain from (
9.2
) that the following identity holds:

(9.4)

D_{\eta}^{3}f=\eta D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)+3D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big).

Hence, for
\mathrm{L}_{4}
, it follows from the above and integration by parts that

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{4}

\displaystyle=6m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r+2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m+1}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=(5m-m^{2})\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r-2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta D_{\eta}\zeta\eta^{m+1}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r\geq 0,

which, combined with (
9.3
), leads to

(9.5)

\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}^{3}f|^{2}+m^{2}\Big|D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r.

Next, it follows from (
9.2
) that

(9.6)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\Big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}=\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)+(m+2)\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(\Big|D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}+(m+2)^{2}\Big|\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\underline{2(m+2)\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m-1}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=\mathrm{L}_{5}},

where
\mathrm{L}_{5}
can be handled by integration by parts:

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{5}

\displaystyle=(2-m-m^{2})\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r-2(m+2)\int_{0}^{1}\zeta D_{\eta}\zeta\eta^{m-1}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\geq(2-m-m^{2})\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r.

Hence, (
9.6
), together with the above, implies that

\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\Big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(\Big|D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}+(3m+6)\Big|\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r,

which, along with (
9.5
) and Lemmas
5.1
and
7.8
, leads to

\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{3}f,D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big),\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big),\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}.

4.
First, a direct calculation gives

(9.7)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}^{3}\Big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}^{4}f|^{2}+m^{2}\Big|D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r+\underline{2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{4}fD_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=\mathrm{L}_{6}}.

Then, for
\mathrm{L}_{6}
, note that the following identity holds due to (
9.4
):

D_{\eta}^{4}f=\eta D_{\eta}^{4}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)+4D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big).

Hence, this, together with integration by parts, yields

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{6}

\displaystyle=8m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r+2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m+1}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{4}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=(7m-m^{2})\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r-2m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta D_{\eta}\zeta\eta^{m+1}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r\geq 0,

which, along with (
9.7
), gives

(9.8)

\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}^{3}\Big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(|D_{\eta}^{4}f|^{2}+m^{2}\Big|D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r.

Next, we note that, by (
9.2
),

D_{\eta}\big(\frac{D_{\eta}^{2}f}{\eta}\big)=D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)+2D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)=\eta D_{\eta}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)+4D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big).

Hence, this, together with a direct calculations, implies

(9.9)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\eta D_{\eta}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)+(m+4)D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big(\Big|\eta D_{\eta}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|^{2}+(m+4)^{2}\Big|D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\underline{2(m+4)\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m+1}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=\mathrm{L}_{7}},

where
\mathrm{L}_{7}
can be handled by integration by parts:

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{7}

\displaystyle=-(m^{2}+5m+4)\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-2(m+4)\int_{0}^{1}\zeta D_{\eta}\zeta\eta^{m+1}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\geq-(m^{2}+5m+4)\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r.

Therefore, (
9.9
), combined with the above, implies

\Big|\zeta(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\geq(3m+12)\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{2}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\Big|D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r,

which, together with (
9.8
) and Lemmas
5.1
and
7.8
, leads to

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{4}f,D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{f}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}\big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}(\frac{f}{\eta})\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{3}\big(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}\big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}(D_{\eta}f+\frac{mf}{\eta})\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}.

This completes the proof.
∎

9.1.
Tangential estimates of the velocity

This section is devoted to establishing the tangential estimates for
U
. We first give time-spatial estimates for the velocity, which will be frequently used in the rest of this section.

Lemma 9.2
.

For any
\iota
satisfying

\iota\in\big(-\frac{\beta}{2},1+\frac{\beta}{2}\big),

and any
a\in(0,1)
, there exists a constant
C(\iota,a,T)>0
such that

\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota-\frac{\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U(t)\big|_{\infty}\leq C(\iota,a,T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota}(D_{\eta}U,U_{t})(t)\big|_{2}\big)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Besides, for any such
\iota\in(-\frac{\beta}{2},1+\frac{\beta}{2})
,
a\in(0,1)
, and
\sigma>0
, there exists a constant
C(\sigma,\iota,a,T)>0
such that

\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota-\beta+\sigma}D_{\eta}U(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(\sigma,\iota,a,T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota}(D_{\eta}U,U_{t})(t)\big|_{2}\big)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

Integrating
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{1}\times\eta_{r}
over
[r,1]
(
r\in(0,1)
) with respect to the radial coordinate gives

(9.10)

D_{\eta}U=\frac{A}{2\mu}\varrho^{\gamma-1}+\frac{m}{\varrho}\int_{r}^{1}\frac{\tilde{r}^{m}\rho_{0}}{\eta^{m}}\big(\frac{D_{\eta}U}{\eta}-\frac{U}{\eta^{2}}\big)\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}-\frac{1}{2\mu\varrho}\int_{r}^{1}\frac{\tilde{r}^{m}}{\eta^{m}}\rho_{0}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}.

Hence, due to
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
and Lemmas
5.1
and
8.1
, for all
r\in[a,1)
and
\iota\in(-\frac{\beta}{2},1+\frac{\beta}{2})
,

(9.11)

\displaystyle|D_{\eta}U|

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}+\frac{C(a,T)}{\rho_{0}}\int_{r}^{1}\rho_{0}\left(|U|+|D_{\eta}U|+|U_{t}|\right)\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}+\frac{C(a,T)}{\rho_{0}}\Big(\int_{r}^{1}\rho_{0}^{2-2\iota}\,\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{a}^{1}\rho_{0}^{2\iota}\left(|U|^{2}+|D_{\eta}U|^{2}+|U_{t}|^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}+C(\iota,a,T)\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta}{2}-\iota}\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota}(U,D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2},

which, along with Lemma
6.1
, yields

\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota-\frac{\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}\leq C(\iota,T)+C(\iota,a,T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota}(D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}\big).

Moreover, using the facts that

\gamma-1+\iota-\beta\geq-\frac{\beta}{2},\qquad\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r,

we also see that, for all
\sigma>0
,

\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota-\beta+\sigma}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\leq C(\sigma,\iota,T)+C(\sigma,\iota,a,T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}_{a}\rho_{0}^{\iota}(D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}\big).

This completes the proof.
∎

9.1.1.
Zeroth-order tangential estimates

The zeroth-order tangential estimate for

U
is exactly given by Lemma
4.1
.
For the case of use, we also present some lower-order estimates for
U
here, which are due to Lemmas
5.1
,
6.1
, and
7.6
.

Lemma 9.3
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C_{0}\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Moreover, for any
p\in(0,\infty)
,
\iota\in(-\frac{1}{p},\infty)
, and
a\in(0,1)
, there exist two positive constants
C(p,\iota,a,T)
and
C(a,T)
such that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\big|\chi_{a}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\iota\beta}U(t)\big|_{p}\leq C(p,\iota,a,T),\qquad|\zeta_{a}U(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\Big|\zeta_{a}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|\zeta_{a}U|_{\infty}^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(a,T).

9.1.2.
First-order tangential estimates

Lemma 9.4
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
We give some auxiliary estimates. First,
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{1}
, combined with (
4.1
), yields

(9.12)

D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)=\frac{1}{2\mu}U_{t}-\frac{1}{2\mu}(V-U)D_{\eta}U+\frac{A\gamma}{4\mu^{2}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V-U).

Then it follows from the above, Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
,
9.1
, and
9.3
, and the Hölder inequality that

(9.13)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}U_{t}|_{2}+|\zeta_{a}(V,U)|_{\infty}\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\zeta_{a}(V,U)|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big(|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}U_{t}|_{2}+(1+|\zeta_{a}U|_{\infty})\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}+1\big).

Next, rewrite (
9.12
) as

(9.14)

D_{\eta}^{2}U=\frac{1}{2\mu}U_{t}-\frac{m}{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U-\frac{U}{\eta}\big)-\frac{1}{2\mu}(V-U)D_{\eta}U+\frac{A\gamma}{4\mu^{2}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V-U).

Multiplying the above by
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}
(
\varepsilon>0
), we obtain from Lemmas
4.13
,
5.1
,
6.2
,
7.8
,
9.2
(with
\iota=\frac{1}{2}
), and
9.3
, and the Hölder inequality that

(9.15)

\!\!\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}&\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta+\varepsilon}{2}}(V,U)\big|_{2}(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}+1)\big)\\
&\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty}^{\frac{\varepsilon}{2\beta}}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}+1\big)\\
&\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}+1\big).\end{aligned}

2.
First, multiplying
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{1}
by
\eta^{m}\eta_{r}
, along with (
4.1
), we have

r^{m}\rho_{0}U_{t}+\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\frac{(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\gamma}}{(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\gamma-1}}(V-U)=2\mu\Big(r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{D_{\eta}U}{\eta_{r}}\Big)_{r}-2\mu mr^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{U}{\eta^{2}}.

Then multiplying the above by
U_{t}
gives

(9.16)

\displaystyle\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(|D_{\eta}U|^{2}+m\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)_{t}+r^{m}\rho_{0}U_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\mu\Big(r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{D_{\eta}UU_{t}}{\eta_{r}}\Big)_{r}-\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\frac{(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\gamma}}{(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\gamma-1}}(V-U)U_{t}-2\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big((D_{\eta}U)^{3}+m\frac{U^{3}}{\eta^{3}}\Big).

Integrating (
9.16
) over
I
, we obtain

(9.17)

\displaystyle\mu\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{2}+m\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U}{\eta}\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\gamma}}{(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\gamma-1}}(V-U)U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r-2\mu\int_{0}^{1}r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big((D_{\eta}U)^{3}+m\frac{U^{3}}{\eta^{3}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=1}^{2}\mathrm{J}_{i}.

2.1. Estimate for
\mathrm{J}_{1}
.
For
\mathrm{J}_{1}
, it follows from Lemmas
6.2
,
6.6
, and
9.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(9.18)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta+\zeta^{\sharp})\frac{(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\gamma}}{(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\gamma-1}}(V-U)U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\zeta(V,U)|_{2}|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty}^{\frac{2\gamma-1}{2\beta}}+|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}(V,U)|_{2}|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty}^{\frac{2\gamma-1-2\beta}{2\beta}}\big)\Big|\frac{r^{(\gamma-\frac{1}{2})m}}{(\eta^{m}\eta_{r})^{\gamma-1}}\Big|_{\infty}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(|\zeta V|_{\infty}+1)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

2.2. Estimate for
\mathrm{J}_{2}
.
To estimate
\mathrm{J}_{2}
, we first divide it into three parts:

(9.19)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{2}

\displaystyle=-2\mu\int_{0}^{1}\zeta r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big((D_{\eta}U)^{3}+m\frac{U^{3}}{\eta^{3}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r-2\mu\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{\sharp}r^{m}\rho_{0}|D_{\eta}U|^{3}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-2\mu m\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{\sharp}r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{U^{3}}{\eta^{3}}\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathrm{J}_{2,i}.

For
\mathrm{J}_{2,1}
, note that, due to
\frac{2m+1}{4}\geq\frac{m}{2}
and Lemmas
7.8
and
A.5
, for any
a\in(0,1)
,

(9.20)

\Big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}^{2}\leq C(a)\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta},D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}.

Hence, due to
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
, (
9.13
), and the Hölder and Young inequalities, we have

(9.21)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{2,1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\zeta\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|\zeta\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta},U_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(T)\Big|\zeta\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\big((1+|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}U|_{\infty})\big|\zeta_{\frac{3}{4}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1\!+\!|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}U|_{\infty}^{2}\!+\!\Big|\zeta\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)\Big(1\!+\!\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)\!+\!\frac{1}{8}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

For
\mathrm{J}_{2,2}
, it follows from integration by parts, (
2.1
), and (
4.1
) that

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{2,2}

\displaystyle=-2\mu\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta^{\sharp}\eta^{m}\varrho|D_{\eta}U|^{2})\eta_{r}D_{\eta}U\,\mathrm{d}r=2\mu\int_{0}^{1}D_{\eta}(\zeta^{\sharp}\eta^{m}\varrho|D_{\eta}U|^{2})\eta_{r}U\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(2\mu\big(D_{\eta}\zeta^{\sharp}+\frac{m\zeta^{\sharp}}{\eta}\big)+\zeta^{\sharp}V\Big)U|D_{\eta}U|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\underline{-\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{\sharp}r^{m}\rho_{0}U^{2}|D_{\eta}U|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r}_{\,\leq 0}+4\mu\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{\sharp}r^{m}\rho_{0}U(D_{\eta}U)(D_{\eta}^{2}U)\,\mathrm{d}r\leq\mathrm{J}_{2,21}+\mathrm{J}_{2,22},

where
\mathrm{J}_{2,21}
–
\mathrm{J}_{2,22}
can be handled by using (
9.15
), Lemmas
5.1
,
6.3
,
9.2
(with
\iota=\frac{1}{2}
),
9.3
, and
A.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities: for any fixed
\varepsilon\in(0,\frac{\beta}{2})
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{2,21}&\leq C(T)(|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty}+|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}V|_{\infty})\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}}U\big|_{2}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\\
&\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{16}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{2,22}&\leq C_{0}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta}{4}-\varepsilon}U\big|_{4}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}-\frac{\beta}{4}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{4}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}\big)\\
&\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{16}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.\end{aligned}

Hence, we arrive at

(9.22)

\mathrm{J}_{2,2}\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{8}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

For
\mathrm{J}_{2,3}
, it follows from Lemmas
5.1
and
9.3
that

(9.23)

\mathrm{J}_{2,3}\leq C_{0}\big|\chi^{\sharp}r^{m}\eta^{-3}\big|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}}U\big|_{3}^{3}\leq C(T).

Collecting (
9.19
)–(
9.21
) and (
9.22
)–(
9.23
) gives

(9.24)

\mathrm{J}_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(1+|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}U|_{\infty}^{2}+\Big|\zeta\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)\Big(1+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{4}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

2.3. Close the energy estimate.
Collecting (
9.17
)–(
9.18
) and (
9.24
), and then applying the Grönwall inequality, we obtain from Lemma
9.3
that, for
t\in[0,T]
,

\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)(1+\mathcal{E}(0,U))\leq C(T).

This completes the proof.
∎

9.1.3.
Second-order tangential estimates

Lemma 9.5
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Moreover, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U(t)\big|_{\infty}\leq C(T),\qquad\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\qquad\text{for any $\varepsilon>0$},

\displaystyle\big|\zeta_{a}U(t)\big|_{\infty}+\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\qquad\text{for any $a\in(0,1)$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
First, since
\frac{m}{2}\leq 1
and
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
, it follows from Lemmas
5.1
,
7.8
,
9.3
–
9.4
, and
A.3
–
A.4
that, for all
a\in(0,1)
,

(9.25)

\displaystyle|\zeta_{a}U|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\zeta_{a}U|_{2}|((\zeta_{a})_{r}U,\zeta_{a}U_{r})|_{2}\leq C(a,T)(1+|\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}U|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big(1+\big|r(\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}U,(\zeta_{a})_{r}D_{\eta}U,\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big(1+\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U,\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(a,T)\big(1+\big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\big),

where we have also used the fact that

|(\zeta_{a})_{r}|\leq C(a)\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}\qquad\text{for all $r\in\bar{I}$}.

Next, it follows from
\frac{m+3}{4}>\frac{m}{2}
and Lemmas
9.4
and
A.4
that

\displaystyle\Big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C(a)\Big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{m+3}{4}}\Big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta},D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\Big(\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big).

Consequently, (
9.13
), together with the above, (
9.25
), and Lemma
9.4
, gives

\displaystyle\Big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}+\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+|\zeta_{a}U|_{\infty}+1\big)\leq C(a,T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}+1\big),

which, along with the Young inequality and (
9.25
), implies that, for all
a\in(0,1)
,

(9.26)

|\zeta_{a}U|_{\infty}^{2}+\Big|\chi_{a}r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}+\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a,T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+1\big).

Besides, we obtain from (
9.15
) and Lemmas
7.8
,
9.2
(with
\iota=\frac{1}{2}
), and
9.4
that, for any
\varepsilon>0
,

(9.27)

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+1\big).

2.

First, multiplying
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{1}
by
\eta^{m}\eta_{r}
, and then applying
\partial_{t}
to the resulting equality, along with
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La}_{1}
and (
4.1
), yield

(9.28)

\displaystyle r^{m}\rho_{0}U_{tt}-A\gamma\Big(\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big)_{r}\eta^{m}+\frac{mA\gamma}{2\mu}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}(V-U)\frac{U}{\eta}

\displaystyle=2\mu\Big(r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{D_{\eta}U_{t}}{\eta_{r}}\Big)_{r}-2\mu mr^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta^{2}}-4\mu\Big(r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{|D_{\eta}U|^{2}}{\eta_{r}}\Big)_{r}+4\mu mr^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{3}}.

Next, multiplying the above by
U_{t}
, we obtain

(9.29)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(r^{m}\rho_{0}U_{t}^{2})_{t}+2\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(|D_{\eta}U_{t}|^{2}+m\frac{U_{t}^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\Big(2\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{D_{\eta}U_{t}U_{t}}{\eta_{r}}-4\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{|D_{\eta}U|^{2}U_{t}}{\eta_{r}}+A\gamma\eta^{m}\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)U_{t}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle-A\gamma\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\big(D_{\eta}U_{t}+\frac{mU_{t}}{\eta}\big)-\frac{mA\gamma}{2\mu}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}(V-U)\frac{U}{\eta}U_{t}

\displaystyle+4\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(|D_{\eta}U|^{2}D_{\eta}U_{t}+m\frac{U^{2}U_{t}}{\eta^{3}}\Big).

Hence, integrating (
9.29
) over
I
leads to

(9.30)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+2\mu\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+2\mu m\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=-A\gamma\int_{0}^{1}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\big(D_{\eta}U_{t}+\frac{mU_{t}}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-\frac{mA\gamma}{2\mu}\int_{0}^{1}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}(V-U)\frac{U}{\eta}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+4\mu\int_{0}^{1}r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(|D_{\eta}U|^{2}D_{\eta}U_{t}+m\frac{U^{2}U_{t}}{\eta^{3}}\Big)\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=3}^{5}\mathrm{J}_{i}.

2.1. Estimate for
\mathrm{J}_{3}
.
For
\mathrm{J}_{3}
, it follows from (
2.1
), Lemmas
4.13
and
9.4
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(9.31)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t}+\frac{mU_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)+\frac{\mu}{8}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

2.2. Estimate for
\mathrm{J}_{4}
.
For
\mathrm{J}_{4}
, it follows from (
9.26
), the fact that
\beta\leq\gamma-1
, Lemmas
4.13
,
5.1
,
6.3
,
6.6
, and
9.3
–
9.4
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(9.32)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{4}

\displaystyle=-\frac{mA\gamma}{2\mu}\int_{0}^{1}(\zeta+\zeta^{\sharp})\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}(V-U)\frac{U}{\eta}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\zeta(V,U)|_{\infty}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U}{\eta}\Big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(T)\big(|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1-\beta}|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}V|_{\infty}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{2\gamma-1}{4}}U\big|_{4}^{2}\big)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{4}+1\big).

2.3. Estimate for
\mathrm{J}_{5}
.
To estimate
\mathrm{J}_{5}
, we first divide it into two parts:

(9.33)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{5}

\displaystyle=4\mu\int_{0}^{1}\chi r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(|D_{\eta}U|^{2}D_{\eta}U_{t}+m\frac{U^{2}U_{t}}{\eta^{3}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+4\mu\int_{0}^{1}\chi^{\sharp}r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(|D_{\eta}U|^{2}D_{\eta}U_{t}+m\frac{U^{2}U_{t}}{\eta^{3}}\Big):=\sum_{i=1}^{2}\mathrm{J}_{5,i}.

Then, for
\mathrm{J}_{5,1}
–
\mathrm{J}_{5,2}
, we obtain from the fact that
\frac{3\beta}{2}>\frac{1}{2}
, (
9.26
)–(
9.27
), Lemmas
5.1
,
9.3
–
9.4
, and
A.4
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(9.34)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{5,1}&\leq C_{0}|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2\beta}}\Big|\chi r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}^{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{4}+1\big)+\frac{\mu}{8}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

(9.35)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{5,2}&\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{4}}U\big|_{4}^{2}\big)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}(U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\beta}{2}}(D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}+1\big)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}(U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+1\big)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}(U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{4}+1\big)+\frac{\mu}{8}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.\end{aligned}

Collecting (
9.33
)–(
9.35
) thus leads to

(9.36)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{5}\leq C(T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{4}+1\big)+\frac{\mu}{4}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

2.4. Close the energy estimate.
Collecting (
9.30
)–(
9.32
) and (
9.36
), and applying the Grönwall inequality, we obtain from Lemma
9.4
that, for
t\in[0,T]
,

\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)(1+\mathcal{E}(0,U))\leq C(T).

Finally, this, together with (
9.26
)–(
9.27
), yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}D_{\eta}U(t)\big|_{\infty}\leq C(T),\qquad\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\qquad\text{for any $\varepsilon>0$},

\displaystyle\big|\zeta_{a}U(t)\big|_{\infty}+\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\qquad\text{for any $a\in(0,1)$}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
9.5
.
∎

9.1.4.
Third-order tangential estimates

We first establish the interior
L^{2}
-energy estimates for
(D_{\eta}V,\frac{V}{\eta})
.

Lemma 9.6
.

For any
a\in(0,1)
, there exists a constant
C(a,T)>0
such that

\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}V,\frac{V}{\eta}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a,T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, since
\frac{m}{2}\leq 1
, it follows from Lemmas
9.4
–
9.5
and
A.4
that, for all
a\in(0,1)
,

(9.37)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\chi_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a)\Big|\chi_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}r\Big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta},D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U,\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U}{\eta},\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}D_{\eta}^{2}U,\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a,T).

Next, multiplying (
4.2
) by
\zeta_{a}^{2}r^{m-2}V
and applying
\zeta_{a}^{2}r^{m}V_{r}\partial_{r}
to (
4.2
), respectively, and then combining these two resulting equations yield

\displaystyle\zeta_{a}^{2}r^{m}\big(V_{r}^{2}+\frac{V^{2}}{r^{2}}\big)_{t}+\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\zeta_{a}^{2}r^{m}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\big(V_{r}^{2}+\frac{V^{2}}{r^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{A\gamma}{\mu}\zeta_{a}^{2}r^{m}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\big(V_{r}U_{r}+\frac{UV}{r^{2}}\big)-\frac{A\gamma(\gamma-1)}{2\mu^{2}}\zeta_{a}^{2}r^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-1}V_{r}(V-U)^{2}.

Integrating the above over
I
, then we obtain from (
9.37
), Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
,
7.8
, and
9.4
–
9.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}V_{r}\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}V\big|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(V_{r},\frac{V}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|\big(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|\zeta_{a}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}V_{r}\big|_{2}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\big|_{2}\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}(V,U)\big|_{\infty}^{2}\leq C(a,T)\big(\big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}V_{r}\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}V\big|_{2}^{2}\big),

which, along with Lemma
5.1
and the Grönwall inequality, leads to

\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}V,\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(V_{r},\frac{V}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a,T)\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((v_{0})_{r},\frac{v_{0}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a,T).

Here, to control the initial value
v_{0}
, it suffices to see

\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((\log\rho_{0})_{rr},\frac{(\log\rho_{0})_{r}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a)\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{rr},|(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}|^{2},\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a),

due to (
1.17
) and Lemma
B.1
.
∎

Lemma 9.7
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

In addition, for any
t\in[0,T]
,
a\in(0,1)
, and
\varepsilon>0
satisfying

0<\varepsilon<\min\big\{\beta,\frac{3\beta-1}{2}\big\},

the following estimates also hold
:

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U(t)\big|_{2}+\Big|\big(U,D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)(t)\Big|_{\infty}\leq C(T),\qquad\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-\varepsilon}D_{\eta}U(t)\big|_{\infty}\leq C(\varepsilon,T),

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{3}U,D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big),\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(a,T).

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
We first give some auxiliary estimates associated with the third-order derivatives of
U
.

1.1.
First, it follows from (
9.37
) and Lemmas
7.8
,
9.5
, and
A.3
–
A.4
that, for all
a\in(0,1)
,

(9.38)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\zeta_{a}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big(\Big|(\zeta_{a})_{r}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+\Big|\zeta_{a}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big(1+\big|r\big(\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}^{2}U,(\zeta_{a})_{r}D_{\eta}^{2}U,\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C(a,T)\Big(1+\Big|r\Big(\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big),(\zeta_{a})_{r}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big),\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\Big(1+\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{3}U,D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\Big).

Next, based on (
9.12
), we can obtain from Lemmas
4.13
,
7.8
,
9.4
, and
9.6
that, for all
a\in(0,1)
,

(9.39)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big(\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}+\Big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\frac{V}{\eta},\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}|(\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}U,\varrho^{\gamma-1})|_{\infty}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\Big(\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}+|\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}+1\Big).

On the other hand, applying
D_{\eta}
to (
9.12
), along with (
4.1
), yields

(9.40)

\displaystyle D_{\eta}^{2}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\mu}D_{\eta}U_{t}-\frac{1}{2\mu}(V-U)D_{\eta}^{2}U-\frac{1}{2\mu}(D_{\eta}V-D_{\eta}U)D_{\eta}U

\displaystyle+\frac{A\gamma}{4\mu^{2}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(D_{\eta}V-D_{\eta}U)+\frac{A\gamma(\gamma-1)}{8\mu^{3}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V-U)^{2},
