which, along with Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
,
7.8
, and
9.4
–
9.6
, leads to

(9.41)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}(V,U)\big|_{\infty}|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(T)|(\varrho^{\gamma-1},\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}U)|_{\infty}\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}r^{\frac{m}{2}}(D_{\eta}V,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(T)|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\zeta_{\frac{1+3a}{4}}(V,U)\big|_{\infty}|\zeta_{a}(V,U)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\big(|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}|_{2}+|\zeta_{a}D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}+1\big).

Collecting (
9.38
)–(
9.39
)
and (
9.41
), together with the Young inequality and Lemma
9.1
, we thus obtain that, for all
a\in(0,1)
,

(9.42)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta_{a}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}^{2}+\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{3}U,D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big),\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(a,T)\Big(\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big)\leq C(a,T)\Big(\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big).

1.2.
Since
\frac{3\beta}{2}>\frac{1}{2}
, it follows from Lemmas
7.8
,
9.2
(with
\iota=\frac{\beta}{2}
),
9.3
–
9.5
, and
A.3
–
A.4
and the Hölder inequality that

(9.43)

\displaystyle\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\big(U,D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)|\chi^{\sharp}(D_{\eta}U,U)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)(|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}+|\chi^{\sharp}(D_{\eta}U,U)|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(1+|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\eta}U|_{\infty})\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\beta}{2}}(D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big).

Moreover, taking
\iota=\frac{\beta}{2}-\varepsilon
in Lemma
9.2
with

0<\varepsilon<\min\big\{\beta,\frac{3\beta-1}{2}\big\},

and using an argument similar to (
9.43
), we can also obtain the following estimate:

(9.44)

\displaystyle|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-\varepsilon}D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\beta-2\varepsilon}{2}}(D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big).

Finally, multiplying (
9.14
) by
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}
and choosing a fixed constant
\sigma>0
such that

\max\{0,1-\beta\}<\sigma<\min\{2\beta,1+\beta\},

we obtain from (
2.1
), (
9.43
)–(
9.44
), and Lemmas
4.13
,
5.1
,
6.2
–
6.3
, and
9.3
–
9.5
that

(9.45)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta+\sigma}{2}}(V,U)\big|_{2}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta-\sigma}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}\big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{2\gamma-1-2\beta}{2}}\big|_{2}|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}V|_{\infty}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{2\gamma-1}{2}}\big|_{2}|\chi^{\sharp}U|_{\infty}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big).

2.
Now, we come to establish the third-order tangential estimates for
U
. First, multiplying (
9.28
) by
U_{tt}
, along with (
2.1
) and (
4.1
), gives

(9.46)

\displaystyle\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(|D_{\eta}U_{t}|^{2}+m\frac{U_{t}^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)_{t}+r^{m}\rho_{0}U_{tt}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Big(2\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{(D_{\eta}U_{t})U_{tt}}{\eta_{r}}\Big)_{r}+\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}(V-U)\big(\gamma D_{\eta}U+\frac{m(\gamma-1)U}{\eta}\big)U_{tt}

\displaystyle+A\gamma\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)U_{tt}-2\mu r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(D_{\eta}U|D_{\eta}U_{t}|^{2}+m\frac{UU_{t}^{2}}{\eta^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle-2r^{m}\rho_{0}\big(4\mu D_{\eta}UD_{\eta}^{2}U+(V-U)|D_{\eta}U|^{2}\big)U_{tt}+4\mu m\frac{r^{m}\rho_{0}}{\eta}\big(\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}-|D_{\eta}U|^{2}\big)U_{tt}.

Hence, integrating (
9.46
) over
I
leads to

(9.47)

\displaystyle\mu\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+m\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=A\gamma\int_{0}^{1}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\Big(\frac{1}{2\mu}(V-U)\big(\gamma D_{\eta}U+\frac{m(\gamma-1)U}{\eta}\big)+D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big)U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{1}r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(\mu D_{\eta}U|D_{\eta}U_{t}|^{2}+\mu m\frac{UU_{t}^{2}}{\eta^{3}}+\big(4\mu D_{\eta}UD_{\eta}^{2}U+(V-U)|D_{\eta}U|^{2}\big)U_{tt}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+4\mu m\int_{0}^{1}\frac{r^{m}\rho_{0}}{\eta}\big(\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}-|D_{\eta}U|^{2}\big)U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=6}^{8}\mathrm{J}_{i}.

2.1. Estimate for
\mathrm{J}_{6}
.
For
\mathrm{J}_{6}
, we divide it into two parts:

(9.48)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{6}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\int_{0}^{1}\zeta\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\Big(|V-U|\big(|D_{\eta}U|+\Big|\frac{U}{\eta}\Big|\big)+|D_{\eta}^{2}U|+\Big|D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|\Big)|U_{tt}|\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+C_{0}\int_{0}^{1}\!\zeta^{\sharp}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\Big(|V-U|\big(|D_{\eta}U|\!+\!\Big|\frac{U}{\eta}\Big|\big)\!+\!|D_{\eta}^{2}U|\!+\!\Big|D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|\Big)|U_{tt}|\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=1}^{2}\mathrm{J}_{6,i}.

For
\mathrm{J}_{6,1}
, it follows from (
2.1
), Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
, and
9.4
–
9.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(9.49)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{6,1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\zeta(V,U)|_{\infty}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{16}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.

For
\mathrm{J}_{6,2}
, we can obtain from the fact that
\gamma-\beta-\frac{1}{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}
, (
2.1
), (
9.43
), (
9.45
), and Lemmas
5.1
,
6.3
, and
9.3
–
9.4
that

(9.50)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{6,2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}(V,U)|_{\infty}+|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty})\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\beta-\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{4}\big)+\frac{1}{16}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.

Hence, collecting (
9.48
)–(
9.50
), we arrive at

(9.51)

\mathrm{J}_{6}\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{4}\big)+\frac{1}{8}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.

2.2. Estimate for
\mathrm{J}_{7}
.
For
\mathrm{J}_{7}
, we first obtain from the Hölder inequality that

(9.52)

\!\!\!\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{7}&\leq C_{0}\Big|\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+C_{0}|D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}(V-U)|D_{\eta}U|^{2}\big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}:=\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathrm{J}_{7,i}.\end{aligned}

Then, for
\mathrm{J}_{7,1}
–
\mathrm{J}_{7,2}
, it follows from (
9.42
)–(
9.43
), (
9.45
), Lemma
9.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(9.53)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{7,1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big(\Big|\zeta\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}+\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big)\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{4}\Big),

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{7,2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}(|\zeta D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}+|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\eta}U|_{\infty})\big|(\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U,\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{4}\Big)+\frac{1}{16}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.

For
\mathrm{J}_{7,3}
, choosing a fixed constant
\sigma
such that

\max\{0,1-\beta\}<\sigma<\min\{3\beta+1,5\beta-1\},

then we obtain from (
9.42
), (
9.44
), and Lemmas
6.2
,
6.6
, and
9.3
–
9.5
that

(9.54)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{7,3}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\zeta_{\frac{5}{8}}(V,U)|_{\infty}|\zeta D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta+\sigma}{2}}(V,U)\big|_{2}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\beta-\sigma}{4}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{\infty}^{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{4}\Big)+\frac{1}{16}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.

Hence, collecting (
9.52
)–(
9.54
) yields

(9.55)

\mathrm{J}_{7}\leq C(T)\Big(1+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{4}\Big)+\frac{1}{8}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.

2.3. Estimate for
\mathrm{J}_{8}
.
To estimate
\mathrm{J}_{8}
, we first obtain from integration by parts that

(9.56)

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{8}

\displaystyle=4\mu m\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{0}^{1}r^{m}\rho_{0}\big(\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}-|D_{\eta}U|^{2}\big)\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+4\mu m\int_{0}^{1}r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(\frac{3U^{3}}{\eta^{3}}-\frac{U|D_{\eta}U|^{2}}{\eta}-2(D_{\eta}U)^{3}+2D_{\eta}UD_{\eta}U_{t}-\frac{2UU_{t}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle:=\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\widetilde{\mathrm{J}}_{8}+\mathrm{J}_{8,1}.

For
\mathrm{J}_{8,1}
, by (
9.42
)–(
9.43
), Lemma
9.4
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{8,1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}^{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{4}\Big),

which, along with (
9.56
), yields

(9.57)

\mathrm{J}_{8}\leq\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\widetilde{\mathrm{J}}_{8}+C(T)\Big(1+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{4}\Big).

Besides, for
\widetilde{\mathrm{J}}_{8}
, since

\frac{m+3}{4}>\frac{m}{2},\qquad\frac{1+3\beta}{4}>\frac{1}{2},

we can deduce from Lemmas
5.1
,
7.8
,
9.3
–
9.5
, and
A.4
that

\displaystyle\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}\leq C_{0}\Big|\chi r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}+C_{0}\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\chi r^{\frac{m+3}{4}}\Big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta},D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+3\beta}{4}}\Big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta},D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)+C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+3\beta}{4}}(U,D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)+C(T)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}+C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+3\beta}{4}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\leq C(T),

which implies that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(9.58)

\widetilde{\mathrm{J}}_{8}\leq C_{0}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}^{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}.

2.4. Close the energy estimate.
First, collecting (
9.47
), (
9.51
), (
9.55
), and (
9.57
) gives

\displaystyle\mu\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+m\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\widetilde{\mathrm{J}}_{8}+C(T)\Big(1+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{4}\Big).

Thus, it follows from (
9.58
), Lemma
9.5
, and the Grönwall inequality that

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\mathcal{E}(0,U)+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\widetilde{\mathrm{J}}_{8}+1\Big)\leq C(T)\Big(1+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}\Big),

which, along with the Young inequality, leads to

\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Finally, this, together with (
9.42
)–(
9.45
), yields the rest of this lemma.
∎

9.2.
Estimates of the velocity near the symmetric center

Lemma 9.8
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{in}}(s,U)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1. Elliptic estimates.
First, Lemmas
9.3
–
9.5
, and
9.7
lead to

(9.59)

\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

2. Dissipation estimates.
Next, since we have already obtained

(9.60)

\int_{0}^{t}|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}U_{tt}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)

due to Lemma
9.7
, it remains to show that

(9.61)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t},D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{4}U,D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}\big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}(\frac{U}{\eta})\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

2.1. Proof of
\eqref{D-txx/D-xxxx}_{1}
.
First, it follows from (
4.2
), Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
, and
9.7
that

(9.62)

|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}V_{t}|_{2}\leq|\zeta V_{t}|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\zeta(V,U)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Next, applying
\partial_{t}
to (
9.12
), along with
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La}_{1}
, we have

(9.63)

\displaystyle D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t}+\frac{mU_{t}}{\eta}\big)

\displaystyle=\underline{\frac{1}{2\mu}U_{tt}+3D_{\eta}UD_{\eta}^{2}U+m\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{2U}{\eta}\big)D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{9}}

\displaystyle\underline{-\frac{1}{2\mu}(V_{t}-U_{t})D_{\eta}U-\frac{1}{2\mu}(V-U)(D_{\eta}U_{t}-|D_{\eta}U|^{2})}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{10}}

\displaystyle+\underline{\frac{A\gamma}{4\mu^{2}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\Big((V_{t}-U_{t})-(\gamma-1)\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)(V-U)\Big)}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{11}}.

Then we obtain from (
9.59
), (
9.62
), and Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
, and
9.7
that

(9.64)

\displaystyle|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{J}_{9}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}U_{tt}|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}U_{tt}|_{2}+1),

\displaystyle|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{J}_{10}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}(D_{\eta}U,V,U)|_{\infty}\big(|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}(V_{t},U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})|_{2}+|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}|_{2}|D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\leq C(T),

\displaystyle|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{J}_{11}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\Big(|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}(V_{t},U_{t})|_{2}+|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}(V,U)|_{\infty}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big)\leq C(T),

which, along with (
9.63
), implies

\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t}+\frac{mU_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)(|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}U_{tt}|_{2}+1).

Finally, this, together with (
9.60
) and Lemma
9.1
, leads to
\eqref{D-txx/D-xxxx}_{1}
.

2.2. Proof of
\eqref{D-txx/D-xxxx}_{2}
.
We first show that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

(9.65)

\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}V,\frac{V}{\eta}\big)(t)\Big|_{4}+\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}V,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Indeed, due to the fact that
\frac{m+3}{4}>\frac{m}{2}
and Lemmas
7.8
,
9.6
–
9.7
, and
A.4
, we have

(9.66)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}V,\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|r^{\frac{m+3}{4}}\Big(\zeta D_{\eta}V,\zeta\frac{V}{\eta},\zeta_{r}D_{\eta}V,\zeta_{r}\frac{V}{\eta},\zeta D_{\eta}^{2}V,\zeta D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}V,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big),

\displaystyle\Big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m}{4}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m+3}{4}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}^{3}U,D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Then, by (
4.1
)–(
4.2
), we have

\displaystyle\Big(D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}V+\frac{mV}{\eta}\big)\Big)_{t}+\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\varrho^{\gamma-1}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}V+\frac{mV}{\eta}\big)

\displaystyle=\underline{-2D_{\eta}UD_{\eta}^{2}V-m\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{U}{\eta}\big)D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V}{\eta}\big)-D_{\eta}^{2}UD_{\eta}V-mD_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\frac{V}{\eta}}_{:=\mathrm{\mathrm{J}}_{12}}

\displaystyle+\underline{\frac{A\gamma(\gamma-1)}{4\mu^{2}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V-U)\Big(\big(3D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)-\big(3D_{\eta}V+\frac{mV}{\eta}\big)\Big)}_{:=\mathrm{\mathrm{J}}_{13}}

\displaystyle+\underline{\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\varrho^{\gamma-1}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)-\frac{A\gamma(\gamma-1)^{2}}{8\mu^{3}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V-U)^{3}}_{:=\mathrm{\mathrm{J}}_{14}}.

Hence, solving the above ODE yields

(9.67)

\displaystyle D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}V+\frac{mV}{\eta}\big)=e^{Q(t)}\big((v_{0})_{r}+\frac{mv_{0}}{r}\big)_{r}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{Q(t)-Q(\tau)}\sum_{i=12}^{14}\mathrm{\mathrm{J}}_{i}\,\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\implies\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}V+\frac{mV}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((v_{0})_{r}+\frac{mv_{0}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{i=12}^{14}|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{\mathrm{J}}_{i}|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}\tau,

where

Q(t):=-\int_{0}^{t}\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\,\mathrm{d}s.

By (
9.59
), (
9.66
), Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
, and
9.6
–
9.7
, and the Hölder inequality, we have

(9.68)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{\mathrm{J}}_{12}|_{2}&\leq C_{0}\Big|\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}V,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(D_{\eta}V,\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{4}\Big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m}{4}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{4}\\
&\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}V,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{\mathrm{J}}_{13}|_{2}&\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}(V,U)|_{\infty}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta},D_{\eta}V,\frac{V}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T),\\
|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{\mathrm{J}}_{14}|_{2}&\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\Big(\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}+|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}(V,U)|_{\infty}^{3}\Big)\leq C(T),\end{aligned}

and, for the initial data, by
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
and Lemma
B.1
, we have

(9.69)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((v_{0})_{r}+\frac{mv_{0}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{in}}(0,U)+C_{0}\|\zeta\nabla_{\bm{y}}^{3}\log\rho_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}+C_{0}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\nabla_{\bm{y}}^{j}\rho_{0}^{\beta}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla_{\bm{y}}\rho_{0}^{\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}^{3-j}\leq C_{0}.

Thus, plugging (
9.68
)–(
9.69
) into
\eqref{DDV}_{2}
, together with the Grönwall inequality, Lemma
9.1
, and (
9.66
), implies
claim (
9.65
).

Now, applying
D_{\eta}
to (
9.40
), along with (
4.1
), we have

\displaystyle D_{\eta}^{3}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)

\displaystyle=\underline{\frac{1}{2\mu}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}-\frac{1}{2\mu}(D_{\eta}^{2}V-3D_{\eta}^{2}U)D_{\eta}U-\frac{1}{\mu}D_{\eta}VD_{\eta}^{2}U}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{15}}

\displaystyle\underline{-\frac{1}{2\mu}(V-U)D_{\eta}^{3}U+\frac{A\gamma}{4\mu^{2}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(D_{\eta}^{2}V-D_{\eta}^{2}U)}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{16}}

\displaystyle+\underline{\frac{A\gamma(\gamma-1)}{8\mu^{3}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}(V-U)\Big(3(D_{\eta}V-D_{\eta}U)+\frac{\gamma-1}{2\mu}(V-U)^{2}\Big)}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{17}}.

Then, based on the above, we obtain from (
9.59
), (
9.65
),
\eqref{vr-urr-l4}_{2}
, Lemmas
4.13
,
6.6
, and
9.7
, and the Hölder inequality that

(9.70)

\displaystyle|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{J}_{15}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}|D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}\big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}(D_{\eta}^{2}V,D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{4}}D_{\eta}V|_{4}\big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m}{4}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{4}\leq C(T)(|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}|_{2}+1),

\displaystyle|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{J}_{16}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}(V,U)|_{\infty}|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{3}U|_{2}+C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}(D_{\eta}^{2}V,D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}\leq C(T),

\displaystyle|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{J}_{17}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}(V,U)\big|_{\infty}\big(\big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}(D_{\eta}V,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}+\big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}(V,U)\big|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\leq C(T).

Next, multiplying (
9.12
) by
\frac{1}{\eta}
and then applying
D_{\eta}
to the resulting equality, together with (
4.1
), yield

\displaystyle D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle=\underline{\frac{1}{2\mu}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)-\frac{1}{2\mu}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V-U}{\eta}\big)D_{\eta}U-\frac{1}{2\mu}\frac{V-U}{\eta}D_{\eta}^{2}U}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{18}}

\displaystyle+\underline{\frac{A\gamma(\gamma-1)}{8\mu^{3}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\frac{(V-U)^{2}}{\eta}+\frac{A\gamma}{4\mu^{2}}\varrho^{\gamma-1}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V-U}{\eta}\big)}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{19}}.

Similar to the calculation of (
9.70
), we can derive from the above and Lemma
9.6
that

\displaystyle|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{J}_{18}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\zeta_{a}r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}^{2}U\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{4}}\frac{V}{\eta}\Big|_{4}\big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}r^{\frac{m}{4}}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{4}\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big),

\displaystyle|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{J}_{19}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big(\big|\chi_{\frac{5}{8}}(V,U)\big|_{\infty}+1\big)\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\frac{V}{\eta},\frac{U}{\eta},D_{\eta}\big(\frac{V}{\eta}\big),D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Therefore, it follows from the above and (
9.70
) that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(9.71)

\displaystyle\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{3}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t},D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big),

which, along with Lemmas
9.1
and
\eqref{D-txx/D-xxxx}_{1}
, leads to
\eqref{D-txx/D-xxxx}_{2}
.
∎

9.3.
Estimates of the velocity away from origin

This subsection is devoted to establishing the following estimates:

Lemma 9.9
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{ex}}(t,U)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{ex}}(s,U)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

This proof will be fulfilled by §
9.3.1
–§
9.3.3
.

9.3.1.
Some preliminaries

In what follows, we choose parameter
\varepsilon_{0}

satisfying (
2.3
), that is,

\begin{cases}\displaystyle 0<\varepsilon_{0}<\min\big\{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2\beta},\frac{\gamma-1}{\beta}-1,\frac{1}{2}\big\}&\displaystyle\quad\text{for }\beta\in\big(\frac{1}{3},\gamma-1\big),\\[6.0pt]
\displaystyle 0<\varepsilon_{0}<\min\big\{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2\beta},\frac{1}{2}\big\}&\quad\text{for }\beta=\gamma-1.\end{cases}

To further simplify our calculations, we define the following quantities:

(9.72)

\Lambda:=D_{\eta}(\varrho^{\beta}),\qquad\psi_{\ell}:=D_{\eta}\log\big(\frac{\eta}{r}\big),\qquad\psi_{h}:=D_{\eta}\log\eta_{r}.

Clearly, (
9.72
), together with (
2.1
) and (
4.1
), also yields

(9.73)

\Lambda=\frac{\varrho^{\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\beta}\eta_{r}}-\beta\varrho^{\beta}(m\psi_{\ell}+\psi_{h}),\qquad\Lambda=\frac{\beta}{2\mu}\varrho^{\beta}(V-U).

Then, by Lemmas
4.13
,
6.3
,

6.6
, and
9.7
, we can derive the following
lemma directly:

Lemma 9.10
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|\Lambda(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\qquad\,\,\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Finally, we summarize the following estimates for case of later analysis, which follow directly from Lemmas
9.3
–
9.5
and
9.7
.

Lemma 9.11
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})(t)\big|_{2}+|(U,D_{\eta}U)(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T).

9.3.2.
Elliptic estimates

The first lemma concerns second-order elliptic estimate. This estimate follows directly from the fact that
(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta>\frac{1}{2}
and Lemma
9.11
.

Lemma 9.12
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Next, we can derive some refined weighted estimates for
U
.

Lemma 9.13
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U(t)\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U(t)\big|_{2}+|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T).

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
L^{2}
-estimates of
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U
.
First, since

\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{1}{2},\qquad\big(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta>\frac{1}{2},

we can choose fixed
(\iota,\sigma)
in Lemma
9.2
such that

\iota+\sigma=\big(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta,\qquad\iota\in\big(-\frac{\beta}{2},1+\frac{\beta}{2}\big),\qquad 0<\sigma<\min\big\{(1-\varepsilon_{0})\beta,\big(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta-\frac{1}{2}\big\}.

Then it follows from Lemmas
7.8
,
9.2
,
9.11
, and
A.4
that

(9.74)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta-\sigma}(D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta-\sigma}(D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(T).

2.
L^{2}
-estimate of
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U
.
First, in view of (
9.73
), (
9.14
) can be rewritten as

(9.75)

D_{\eta}^{2}U=\underline{\frac{1}{2\mu}U_{t}-\frac{m}{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U-\frac{U}{\eta}\big)}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{20}}\ \underline{-\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{\Lambda}{\varrho^{\beta}}D_{\eta}U}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{21}}+\underline{\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu\beta}\varrho^{\gamma-1-\beta}\Lambda}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{22}}.

Then it follows from the facts that

\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r,\qquad\big(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta>0,\qquad\big(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta>\frac{1}{2},\qquad-\big(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta+\gamma-1>0,

(
9.74
), and Lemmas
5.1
,
7.8
,
9.10
–
9.12
, and
A.4
that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(9.76)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{20}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(U,D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(U,D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(\mathrm{J}_{21},\mathrm{J}_{22})\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(D_{\eta}U,\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})\big|_{2}|\Lambda|_{\infty}\leq C(T).

Therefore, combining (
9.75
)–(
9.76
) leads to

(9.77)

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\leq\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(\mathrm{J}_{20},\mathrm{J}_{21},\mathrm{J}_{22})\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

3.
L^{\infty}
-estimate of
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U
.
Using the fact that

\frac{3}{2}\beta>\frac{1}{2},

we obtain from Lemmas
5.1
,
7.8
,
9.11
, and
A.4
that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}\mathrm{J}_{20}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}(U,D_{\eta}U,U_{t})|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\beta}{2}}(U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(T),

\displaystyle|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}(\mathrm{J}_{21},\mathrm{J}_{22})|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(|D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}+|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1})|\chi^{\sharp}\Lambda|_{\infty}\leq C(T).

Therefore, (
9.75
), together with the above, leads to

|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U|_{\infty}\leq|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}(\mathrm{J}_{20},\mathrm{J}_{21},\mathrm{J}_{22})|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
9.13
.
∎

Besides, we have the following estimate for
D_{\eta}\Lambda
.

Lemma 9.14
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}
,

\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}\Lambda|\leq C(T)\chi^{\sharp}\Big(\rho_{0}^{\beta}\big(\int_{0}^{t}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|\,\mathrm{d}s\big)^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|(D_{\eta}^{2}U,\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U)|\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).

Proof.

A direct calculation, together with (
2.1
) and (
9.73
), gives

(9.78)

\displaystyle D_{\eta}\Lambda

\displaystyle=\frac{\varrho^{\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{rr}}{\eta_{r}^{2}\rho_{0}^{\beta}}-\frac{\varrho^{\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\eta_{r}\rho_{0}^{\beta}}\big(2\beta m\psi_{\ell}+(2\beta+1)\psi_{h}\big)

\displaystyle+\beta\varrho^{\beta}\big(\beta(m\psi_{\ell}+\psi_{h})^{2}-(mD_{\eta}\psi_{\ell}+D_{\eta}\psi_{h})\big).

Next, by
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle((\log\eta_{r})_{r})_{t}=(D_{\eta}U)_{r}\implies(\log\eta_{r})_{r}=\int_{0}^{t}(D_{\eta}U)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle\big(\log(\frac{\eta}{r})\big)_{tr}=\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)_{r}\implies\big(\log(\frac{\eta}{r})\big)_{r}=\int_{0}^{t}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}s,

which, along with (
9.72
) and the chain rules, implies

(9.79)

\psi_{h}=\frac{1}{\eta_{r}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{2}U\,\mathrm{d}s,\qquad\psi_{\ell}=\frac{1}{\eta_{r}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s,

and

(9.80)

\displaystyle D_{\eta}\psi_{h}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\psi_{h}}{\eta_{r}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}^{2}U\,\mathrm{d}s+\frac{1}{\eta_{r}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}(\eta_{r}^{2}\psi_{h}D_{\eta}^{2}U+\eta_{r}^{2}D_{\eta}^{3}U)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=-\psi_{h}^{2}+\frac{1}{\eta_{r}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\big(\eta_{r}\psi_{h}(\eta_{r}\psi_{h})_{t}+\eta_{r}^{2}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big)\,\mathrm{d}s=-\frac{1}{2}\psi_{h}^{2}+\frac{1}{\eta_{r}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{2}D_{\eta}^{3}U\,\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle D_{\eta}\psi_{\ell}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\psi_{h}}{\eta_{r}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s+\frac{1}{\eta_{r}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{2}\Big(\psi_{h}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)+D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle=-\psi_{h}\psi_{\ell}+\frac{1}{\eta_{r}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\eta_{r}^{2}\psi_{h}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)+\eta_{r}^{2}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s.

Hence, it follows from Lemmas
5.1
,

7.8
, and
9.7
that

(9.81)

\displaystyle\chi^{\sharp}|\psi_{h}|

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|\,\mathrm{d}s,\quad\chi^{\sharp}|\psi_{\ell}|\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}\psi_{h}|

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{2}+C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}^{3}U|\,\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}\psi_{\ell}|

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|\,\mathrm{d}s+C(T),

which, along with the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\in H^{3}(\frac{1}{2},1)
and Lemma
8.1
, yields the desired estimates of this lemma.
∎

Now, we can establish the third-order elliptic estimate for
U
.

Lemma 9.15
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(\rho_{0}^{-\beta}D_{\eta}\Lambda,D_{\eta}^{3}U)(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
According to Lemmas
7.8
,
9.13
–
9.14
, and
A.4
, the Hölder inequality, and the Minkowski integral inequality such as

\Big|\int_{0}^{t}\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U\,\mathrm{d}s\Big|_{2}\leq\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s,

we have

(9.82)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}\Lambda\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{\infty}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(D_{\eta}^{2}U,\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U)\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).

2.
L^{2}
-estimate of
\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U
.
Applying
D_{\eta}
to (
9.75
), together with (
9.72
), gives

(9.83)

\!\!\begin{aligned} D_{\eta}^{3}U&=\underline{\frac{1}{2\mu}D_{\eta}U_{t}-\frac{m}{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}^{2}U-\frac{2D_{\eta}U}{\eta}+\frac{2U}{\eta^{2}}\big)}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{23}}\\
&\quad\underline{-\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{\Lambda}{\varrho^{\beta}}D_{\eta}^{2}U-\frac{1}{\beta}\big(\frac{D_{\eta}\Lambda}{\varrho^{\beta}}-\frac{\Lambda^{2}}{\varrho^{2\beta}}\big)D_{\eta}U}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{24}}\!\!\!+\underline{\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu\beta}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\big(\frac{D_{\eta}\Lambda}{\varrho^{\beta}}+\frac{\gamma-1-\beta}{\beta}\frac{\Lambda^{2}}{\varrho^{2\beta}}\big)}_{:=\mathrm{J}_{25}}\!\!.\end{aligned}

Then it follows from (
9.82
), the facts that

\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r,\qquad\big(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta>\frac{1}{2},\qquad-\big(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta+\gamma-1>0,

and Lemmas
5.1
,
8.1
, and
9.10
–
9.13
that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{23}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(U,D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{24}\big|_{2}&\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}|\Lambda|_{\infty}\\
&\quad+C(T)\big(|D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}\Lambda\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}|\Lambda|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\\
&\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{25}\big|_{2}&\leq C(T)\big(|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}\Lambda\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon_{0})\beta+\gamma-1}\big|_{2}|\Lambda|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\\
&\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).\end{aligned}

Therefore, the above estimates, together with (
9.83
), gives

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}\leq\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(\mathrm{J}_{23},\mathrm{J}_{24},\mathrm{J}_{25})\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big),

which, along with the Grönwall inequality, implies

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Finally, the estimate on
D_{\eta}\Lambda
follows from the above and (
9.82
).
∎

9.3.3.
Dissipation estimates

We first establish the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
-estimate for
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U_{t}
.

Lemma 9.16
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, applying
\partial_{t}
to (
9.10
), together with
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La}_{1}
, gives

(9.84)

\!\!\begin{aligned} D_{\eta}U_{t}&=-\frac{A(\gamma-1)}{2\mu}\varrho^{\gamma-1}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)+\frac{m}{\varrho}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\int_{r}^{1}\tilde{r}^{m}\rho_{0}\big(\frac{D_{\eta}U}{\eta^{m+1}}-\frac{U}{\eta^{m+2}}\big)\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}\\
&\quad+\frac{m}{\varrho}\int_{r}^{1}\tilde{r}^{m}\rho_{0}\big(\frac{D_{\eta}U_{t}-|D_{\eta}U|^{2}}{\eta^{m+1}}-\frac{(m+1)UD_{\eta}U+U_{t}}{\eta^{m+2}}+\frac{(m+2)U^{2}}{\eta^{m+3}}\big)\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}\\
&\quad-\frac{1}{2\mu\varrho}\Big(\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\int_{r}^{1}\!\frac{\tilde{r}^{m}}{\eta^{m}}\rho_{0}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}+\!\int_{r}^{1}\!\tilde{r}^{m}\rho_{0}\big(\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta^{m}}-\frac{mUU_{t}}{\eta^{m+1}}\big)\,\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}\Big)+|D_{\eta}U|^{2}.\end{aligned}

Then, by following the calculation similar to (
9.11
) in Lemma
9.2
, it follows from
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
, Lemmas
5.1
and
8.1
, and the Hölder inequality that, for all
r\in[\frac{1}{2},1]
,

(9.85)

\displaystyle|D_{\eta}U_{t}|

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(|U|+|D_{\eta}U|)\Big(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}+\frac{1}{\rho_{0}}\int_{r}^{1}\rho_{0}(|U|+|D_{\eta}U|+|U_{t}|)\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{C(T)}{\rho_{0}}\int_{r}^{1}\rho_{0}\big(|U_{t}|+|D_{\eta}U_{t}|+|U_{tt}|+|U||U_{t}|+|U|^{2}+|D_{\eta}U|^{2})\mathrm{d}\tilde{r}+|D_{\eta}U|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}(U,D_{\eta}U)|+|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta-1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U)|\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta-1}{2}}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t},U_{tt},UU_{t},U^{2},|D_{\eta}U|^{2})\big|_{2}+|D_{\eta}U|^{2}.

Using the facts that

\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r,\qquad\big(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta+\gamma-1>(1-\varepsilon_{0})\beta-\frac{1}{2}>-\frac{\beta}{2},

we can obtain from (
9.85
) and Lemmas
9.3
,
9.11
, and
9.13
that

(9.86)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(1-\varepsilon_{0})\beta-\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U,U_{t})\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t},U_{tt})\big|_{2}+|U|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C(T)|(U,D_{\eta}U)|_{\infty}\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(1-\varepsilon_{0})\beta-\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\big).

Next, note that, by Lemmas
7.8
,
9.11
,
9.15
, and
A.4
, we have

(9.87)

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(1-\varepsilon_{0})\beta-\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\beta}{2}}(D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}^{3}U)\big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Therefore, combining (
9.86
)–(
9.87
), along with Lemma
9.7
, leads to the desired estimate.
∎

Now, we establish the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
-estimate for
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}
.

Lemma 9.17
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, it follows from (
4.2
) and Lemmas
4.13
,
6.3
, and
9.11
that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}V_{t}\big|_{2}\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(V,U)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)(|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}V|_{\infty}+|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U|_{2})\leq C(T).

Then we obtain from the above, (
9.63
),
\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{1}{2}
, Lemmas
5.1
,
6.3
, and
9.11
–
9.13
that

\displaystyle\ \begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{9}\big|_{2}&\leq C(T)(|\chi^{\sharp}(U,D_{\eta}U)|_{\infty}+1)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(U_{tt},U,D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U)\big|_{2}\big)\\
&\leq C(T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}+1\big),\end{aligned}

(9.88)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{10}\big|_{2}&\leq C_{0}|D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(V_{t},U_{t})\big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}(V,U)|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}(V,U)|_{\infty}|D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}+1\big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{11}\big|_{2}&\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(V_{t},U_{t})\big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C(T)|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}(V,U)|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\big|_{2}|(U,D_{\eta}U)|_{\infty}\leq C(T),\end{aligned}

which thus yields

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(\mathrm{J}_{9},\mathrm{J}_{10},\mathrm{J}_{11})\big|_{2}+C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}+1\big).

Then this, together with Lemmas
9.11
and
9.16
, leads to the desired estimate.
∎

Finally, we establish the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
-estimate for
\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U
.

Lemma 9.18
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
First, we can show that

(9.89)

|\chi^{\sharp}(\psi_{\ell},\psi_{h},D_{\eta}\Lambda)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).

Indeed, recall from Lemma
9.14
that

\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}\Lambda|\leq C(T)\chi^{\sharp}\Big(\rho_{0}^{\beta}\big(\int_{0}^{t}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|\,\mathrm{d}s\big)^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|(D_{\eta}^{2}U,\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U)|\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).

Then (
9.89
) can be directly obtained from the above, Lemma
9.13
, and the following controls due to Lemmas
7.8
,
9.12
,
9.15
, and
A.3
–
A.4
:

(9.90)

\displaystyle|\chi^{\sharp}(D_{\eta}^{2}U,\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U)|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi^{\sharp}(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}^{3}U)|_{1}+|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U|_{\infty}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\sum_{j=2}^{4}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{j}U\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}+1\big),

where we have used Lemma
A.4
by taking
\vartheta=-\varepsilon_{0}>-\frac{1}{2}
and
\varepsilon=\varepsilon_{0}
.

Next, we claim:

(9.91)

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}\Lambda\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).

Indeed, it follows from the calculations in (
9.78
) that

\displaystyle D_{\eta}^{2}\Lambda

\displaystyle=\frac{\varrho^{\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{rrr}}{\eta_{r}^{3}\rho_{0}^{\beta}}-\frac{\varrho^{\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{rr}}{\eta_{r}^{2}\rho_{0}^{\beta}}\big(3\beta m\psi_{\ell}+(3\beta+3)\psi_{h}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\varrho^{\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\eta_{r}\rho_{0}^{\beta}}\big(3\beta^{2}m^{2}\psi_{\ell}^{2}+3\beta m(2\beta+1)\psi_{\ell}\psi_{h}+(3\beta^{2}+3\beta+1)\psi_{h}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{\varrho^{\beta}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\eta_{r}\rho_{0}^{\beta}}\big(3\beta mD_{\eta}\psi_{\ell}+(3\beta+1)D_{\eta}\psi_{h}\big)

\displaystyle-\beta\varrho^{\beta}\big(\beta^{2}(m\psi_{\ell}+\psi_{h})^{3}-3\beta(m\psi_{\ell}+\psi_{h})(mD_{\eta}\psi_{\ell}+D_{\eta}\psi_{h})+(mD_{\eta}^{2}\psi_{\ell}+D_{\eta}^{2}\psi_{h})\big),

which, along with the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\in H^{3}(\frac{1}{2},1)
, Lemmas
5.1
and
8.1
, and the Young inequality that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}
,

\displaystyle\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}^{2}\Lambda|

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\chi^{\sharp}\big(1+|(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{rrr}|+|(\psi_{\ell},\psi_{h})|^{2}+|(D_{\eta}\psi_{\ell},D_{\eta}\psi_{h})|\big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\beta}\big(|(\psi_{\ell},\psi_{h})|^{3}+|(\psi_{\ell},\psi_{h})||(D_{\eta}\psi_{\ell},D_{\eta}\psi_{h})|+|(D_{\eta}^{2}\psi_{\ell},D_{\eta}^{2}\psi_{h})|\big).

Moreover, a direct calculation, together with (
9.79
)–(
9.80
), gives

\displaystyle D_{\eta}^{2}\psi_{h}

\displaystyle=-\psi_{h}D_{\eta}\psi_{h}-\frac{2\psi_{h}}{\eta_{r}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{2}D_{\eta}^{3}U\,\mathrm{d}s+\frac{2}{\eta_{r}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{3}\psi_{h}D_{\eta}^{3}U\,\mathrm{d}s+\frac{1}{\eta_{r}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{3}D_{\eta}^{4}U\,\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle D_{\eta}^{2}\psi_{\ell}

\displaystyle=-\psi_{h}D_{\eta}\psi_{\ell}-\psi_{\ell}D_{\eta}\psi_{h}-\frac{2\psi_{h}}{\eta_{r}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{2}\psi_{h}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\eta_{r}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{3}(2\psi_{h}^{2}+D_{\eta}\psi_{h})D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s+\frac{3}{\eta_{r}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{3}\psi_{h}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle-\frac{2\psi_{h}}{\eta_{r}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{2}D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s+\frac{1}{\eta_{r}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\eta_{r}^{3}D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s,

which, along with (
9.81
), Lemmas
5.1
,
7.8
, and
9.11
, and the Young inequality that

\displaystyle\chi^{\sharp}|(D_{\eta}^{2}\psi_{h},D_{\eta}^{2}\psi_{\ell})|

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\big(\int_{0}^{t}\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|\,\mathrm{d}s\big)^{3}+\big(\int_{0}^{t}\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|\,\mathrm{d}s\big)\big(\int_{0}^{t}\chi^{\sharp}|D_{\eta}^{3}U|\,\mathrm{d}s\big)\Big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\chi^{\sharp}|(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}^{3}U,D_{\eta}^{4}U)|\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).

Therefore, it follows from the facts that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\in H^{3}(\frac{1}{2},1)
and
\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{1}{2}
, (
9.90
), Lemmas
9.13
and
9.15
, and the Minkowski integral inequality that

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}\Lambda\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(|\rho_{0}^{\beta}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}}|\chi^{\sharp}(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{rrr}|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}|\rho_{0}^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U|_{\infty}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\int_{0}^{t}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|_{\infty}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\int_{0}^{t}|D_{\eta}^{2}U|_{\infty}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).

This completes the proof of (
9.91
).

2.
Rewrite
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{1}
in view of (
9.72
) as

\varrho^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U+\frac{1}{\beta}D_{\eta}(\varrho^{\beta})D_{\eta}U=-m\varrho^{\beta}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)+\frac{1}{2\mu}\varrho^{\beta}U_{t}+\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu\beta}\varrho^{\gamma-1}D_{\eta}(\varrho^{\beta}).

Applying
D_{\eta}^{2}
to both sides of the above, then dividing the resulting equality by
\varrho^{\beta}
, together with (
9.72
), implies

(9.92)

\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{cross}}:=(D_{\eta}^{3}U)_{r}+\big(\frac{1}{\beta}+2\big)\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\beta})_{r}}{\rho_{0}^{\beta}}D_{\eta}^{3}U=\sum_{i=26}^{29}\mathrm{J}_{i},

where

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{26}

\displaystyle:=(1+2\beta)\eta_{r}(m\psi_{\ell}+\psi_{h})D_{\eta}^{3}U-\big(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\big)\frac{\eta_{r}}{\varrho^{\beta}}D_{\eta}\Lambda D_{\eta}^{2}U-\frac{1}{\beta}\frac{\eta_{r}}{\varrho^{\beta}}D_{\eta}^{2}\Lambda D_{\eta}U,

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{27}

\displaystyle:=-m\frac{\eta_{r}}{\varrho^{\beta}}\Big(D_{\eta}\Lambda D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)+2\Lambda D_{\eta}^{2}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)+\varrho^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{28}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{2\mu}\frac{\eta_{r}}{\varrho^{\beta}}\big(D_{\eta}\Lambda U_{t}+2\Lambda D_{\eta}U_{t}+\varrho^{\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big),

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{29}

\displaystyle:=\frac{A\gamma}{2\mu\beta}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-1-3\beta}\Big(\varrho^{2\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}\Lambda+\frac{3(\gamma-1)}{\beta}\varrho^{\beta}\Lambda D_{\eta}\Lambda+\frac{(\gamma-1)(\gamma-1-\beta)}{\beta^{2}}\Lambda^{3}\Big).

For
\mathrm{J}_{26}
–
\mathrm{J}_{29}
, it follows from the facts that

\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r,\quad\big(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta>\frac{1}{2}>\varepsilon_{0},\quad\beta\leq\gamma-1,\quad\big(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\big)\beta+\gamma-1-3\beta>-\frac{\beta}{2},

(
9.89
), (
9.91
), and Lemmas
5.1
,
7.8
,
8.1
,
9.10
–
9.13
,
9.15
, and
A.4
that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{26}\big|_{2}&\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}(\psi_{\ell},\psi_{h})\big|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}\\[1.42262pt]
&\quad+C(T)\big(|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\eta}\Lambda|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U\big|_{2}\!+\!\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}\Lambda\big|_{2}|D_{\eta}U|_{\infty}\big)\\[1.42262pt]
&\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{27}\big|_{2}&\leq C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}\Lambda\big|_{2}|(U,D_{\eta}U)|_{\infty}\\
&\quad+C(T)(1+|\Lambda|_{\infty})\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(U,D_{\eta}U,D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}^{3}U)\big|_{2}\leq C(T),\end{aligned}

(9.93)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{28}\big|_{2}&\leq C(T)\big(|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\eta}\Lambda|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}U_{t}\big|_{2}+|\Lambda|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big)\\
&\quad+C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)|\chi^{\sharp}D_{\eta}\Lambda|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(U_{t},D_{\eta}U_{t})\big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C(T)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(D_{\eta}U_{t},D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t})\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big|_{2}+1\Big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathrm{J}_{29}\big|_{2}&\leq C(T)|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}\Lambda\big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C(T)|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1-\beta}|\Lambda|_{\infty}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}\Lambda\big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C(T)\underline{(\gamma-1-\beta)\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta+\gamma-1-3\beta}\big|_{2}|\Lambda|_{\infty}^{3}}_{\,(=0,\text{ if $\beta=\gamma-1$})}\\
&\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+1\Big).\end{aligned}

Therefore, it follows from (
9.92
)–(
9.93
) that

\displaystyle\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{cross}}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(\mathrm{J}_{26},\mathrm{J}_{27},\mathrm{J}_{28},\mathrm{J}_{29})\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big|_{2}+1\Big).

Since

\chi^{\sharp}=\zeta-\chi+\zeta^{\sharp},\qquad\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r,

we can obtain from Lemma
7.8
and Lemma
D.1
in Appendix
D
that

\displaystyle\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{3}U\big|_{2}+\big|\zeta^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{cross}}\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big|_{2}+\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)+1\Big),

which, along with Lemmas
9.8
and
9.15
, and the Grönwall inequality, leads to

(9.94)

\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{4}U\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big|_{2}+1\big).

Finally, this, together with Lemma
9.17
, implies the desired result of this lemma.
∎

9.4.
Time-weighted estimates of the velocity

Lemma 9.19
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

t\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt},\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, by (
4.1
), we rewrite (
9.28
) as

(9.95)

\displaystyle r^{m}\rho_{0}U_{tt}-A\gamma\Big(\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big)_{r}\eta^{m}+mA\eta^{m}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}(\varrho^{\gamma})\frac{U}{\eta}

\displaystyle=2\mu\Big(r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{D_{\eta}U_{t}}{\eta_{r}}\Big)_{r}-2\mu mr^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta^{2}}-4\mu\Big(r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{|D_{\eta}U|^{2}}{\eta_{r}}\Big)_{r}+4\mu mr^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{3}}.

Note that the following equality holds in view of
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La}_{1}
:

(\eta^{m}\eta_{r}D_{\eta}(\varrho^{\gamma}))_{t}=-\frac{\gamma}{\beta}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-\beta}\Lambda\big(\gamma D_{\eta}U+\frac{m(\gamma-1)U}{\eta}\big)-\gamma\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big).

Hence, based on the above, we can apply
\partial_{t}
to (
9.95
) and obtain from (
9.72
) that

\displaystyle r^{m}\rho_{0}U_{ttt}+A\gamma\Big(\gamma\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)^{2}+\varrho^{\gamma}\big(|D_{\eta}U|^{2}+\frac{mU^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\big)-\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t}+\frac{mU_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big)_{r}\eta^{m}

\displaystyle-A\gamma m\Big(\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big)_{r}\eta^{m-1}U

\displaystyle-mA\gamma\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-\beta}\Big(\big(\frac{\gamma}{\beta}D_{\eta}U\frac{U}{\eta}+\frac{m(\gamma-1)+1}{\beta}\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}-\frac{U_{t}}{\beta\eta}\big)\Lambda+\varrho^{\beta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\frac{U}{\eta}\Big)

\displaystyle=2\mu\Big(r^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{D_{\eta}U_{tt}}{\eta_{r}}\Big)_{r}-2\mu mr^{m}\rho_{0}\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta^{2}}

\displaystyle+12\mu\Big(\frac{r^{m}\rho_{0}}{\eta_{r}}\big((D_{\eta}U)^{3}-D_{\eta}UD_{\eta}U_{t}\big)\Big)_{r}+12\mu mr^{m}\rho_{0}\Big(\frac{U_{t}U}{\eta^{3}}-\frac{U^{3}}{\eta^{4}}\Big).

Multiplying the above by
U_{tt}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
, we arrive at

(9.96)

\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}+2\mu\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}D_{\eta}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}+2\mu m\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{i=30}^{32}\mathrm{J}_{i},

where

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{30}&:=A\gamma\int_{0}^{1}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\Big(\gamma\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)^{2}+\big(|D_{\eta}U|^{2}+\frac{mU^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\big)\Big)\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt}+\frac{mU_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r\\
&\quad\,\,-A\gamma\int_{0}^{1}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t}+\frac{mU_{t}}{\eta}\big)\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt}+\frac{mU_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r\\
&\quad\,\,-A\gamma m\int_{0}^{1}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt}+\frac{mU_{tt}}{\eta}\big)+D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)U_{tt}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r,\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{31}&:=mA\gamma\int_{0}^{1}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma-\beta}\big(\frac{\gamma}{\beta}D_{\eta}U\frac{U}{\eta}+\frac{m(\gamma-1)+1}{\beta}\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}-\frac{U_{t}}{\beta\eta}\big)\Lambda U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r\\
&\quad\,\,+mA\gamma\int_{0}^{1}\eta^{m}\eta_{r}\varrho^{\gamma}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\frac{U}{\eta}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r,\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{32}&:=12\mu\int_{0}^{1}r^{m}\rho_{0}\Big((D_{\eta}U_{t}-|D_{\eta}U|^{2})D_{\eta}UD_{\eta}U_{tt}+m\big(\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}-\frac{U^{2}}{\eta^{2}}\big)\frac{UU_{tt}}{\eta^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r.\end{aligned}

Then it follows from (
4.1
), Lemmas
4.13
,
5.1
,
6.3
,
6.6
,
9.4
, and
9.7
–
9.11
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{30}&\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\Big|\big(1,D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta},D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt},\!\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C(T)|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\Big|\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big(\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(U,D_{\eta}U)\big|_{2}\Big)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\big)+\frac{\mu}{8}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt},\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

(9.97)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{31}&\leq C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1-\beta}|\Lambda|_{\infty}\Big(\Big|\big(D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U}{\eta}\Big|_{2}+\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\Big|_{2}\Big)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\varrho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}\Big|\frac{U}{\eta}\Big|_{\infty}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U,D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\mathcal{E}(t,U)\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathrm{J}_{32}&\leq C(T)\Big|\big(1,D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{\infty}^{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t},\frac{U_{t}}{\eta},D_{\eta}U,\frac{U}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt},\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)+\frac{\mu}{8}\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt},\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.\end{aligned}

Therefore, (
9.96
)–(
9.97
) lead to

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(t\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\big)+t\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt},\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\big).

Then we integrate the above over
[\tau,t]
and use Lemma
9.7
to obtain

(9.98)

t\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{t}s\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt},\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq\tau\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(\tau)\big|_{2}^{2}+C(T).

Thanks to Lemmas
9.7
and
A.6
, we can find a sequence
\{\tau_{k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
such that

\tau_{k}\to 0,\quad\ \tau_{k}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(\tau_{k})\big|_{2}\to 0\qquad\,\,\text{as $k\to\infty$}.

Taking
\tau=\tau_{k}
in (
9.98
) and then letting
k\to\infty
, we finally obtain

(9.99)

t\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s\Big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(D_{\eta}U_{tt},\frac{U_{tt}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.20
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

t\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{in}}(t,U)\leq C(T)\qquad\,\,\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

Note that, by (
9.64
), (
9.71
), and Lemma
9.19
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U_{t}+\frac{mU_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(1+\sqrt{t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}^{3}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big|_{2}+\sqrt{t}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}D_{\eta}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\eta}\big(D_{\eta}U+\frac{mU}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\sqrt{t}\Big|\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t},D_{\eta}\big(\frac{U_{t}}{\eta}\big)\Big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big).

Hence, this, together with Lemma
9.1
, leads to the desired result of this lemma.
∎

Lemma 9.21
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

t\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{ex}}(t,U)\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, using the same argument as in the proofs of Lemmas
9.16
and
9.19
, we can obtain

\sqrt{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}U_{t}\big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Hence, this, together with (
9.88
), (
9.94
), and Lemma
9.19
, leads to

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}(D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t},D_{\eta}^{4}U)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\sqrt{t}\big|\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}D_{\eta}^{2}U_{t}\big|_{2}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\sqrt{t}\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}+1\big)\leq C(T).

This completes the proof.
∎

10.
Global-In-Time Well-Posedness of the Classical Solutions

Our goal of this section is to prove Theorem
2.1
. We divide the proof into two steps.

1. Existence and Uniqueness of
(U,\eta)
.
According to Theorem
3.1
, there exists a unique classical solution
(U,\eta)(t,r)
of
IBVP
(
2.2
) in
[0,T_{*}]\times\bar{I}
for some
T_{*}>0
, satisfying (
3.2
). Now, suppose that
\overline{T}_{*}>0
is the life span of
(U,\eta)(t,r)
, and
T
is any fixed time satisfying
T\in(0,\overline{T}_{*})
. We claim:

(10.1)

\overline{T}_{*}=\infty.

Otherwise, if
\overline{T}_{*}<\infty
, collecting the uniform
a priori
bounds obtained in Lemmas
5.1
,
7.8
,
9.8
–
9.9
, and
9.20
–
9.21
, we arrive at all the desired global uniform estimates:

(10.2)

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,\overline{T}_{*})}(\mathcal{E}(t,U)+t\mathcal{D}(t,U))+\int_{0}^{\overline{T}_{*}}\mathcal{D}(s,U)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\overline{T}_{*}),

\displaystyle(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})(t,r)\in[C^{-1}(\overline{T}_{*}),C(\overline{T}_{*})]\qquad\text{for all $(t,r)\in[0,\overline{T}_{*})\times\bar{I}$},

where
C(\overline{T}_{*})\in(1,\infty)
is a constant depending only on
(n,\mu,\gamma,A,\beta,\varepsilon_{0},\rho_{0},u_{0},\mathcal{K}_{1},\mathcal{K}_{2},\overline{T}_{*})
. Moreover, from Lemma
C.1
in Appendix
C
,
\eqref{811}_{1}
also leads to

(10.3)

\sup_{t\in[0,\overline{T}_{*})}(\mathring{\mathcal{E}}(t,U)+t\mathring{\mathcal{D}}(t,U))+\int_{0}^{\overline{T}_{*}}\mathring{\mathcal{D}}(s,U)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\overline{T}_{*}),

where
(\mathring{\mathcal{E}},\mathring{\mathcal{D}})(t,U)
are defined in (
C.1
)–(
C.2
) of Appendix
C
.

Consequently, for any sequence
\{t_{k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}\subset[0,\overline{T}_{*})
with
t_{k}\to\overline{T}_{*}
, there exist
both a subsequence
\{t_{k_{\ell}}\}_{\ell=1}^{\infty}
and a limit vector
(U,\eta)(\overline{T}_{*},r)
such that, for
j=0,1
, as
\ell\to\infty
,

(10.4)

\displaystyle\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\partial_{t}^{j}U,\partial_{t}^{j}U_{r},\frac{\partial_{t}^{j}U}{r}\big)(t_{k_{\ell}},r)\to\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\partial^{j}_{t}U,\partial^{j}_{t}U_{r},\frac{\partial^{j}_{t}U}{r}\big)(\overline{T}_{*},r)\qquad\text{weakly in $L^{2}$},

\displaystyle\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\partial_{r}^{j+2}U,\partial_{r}^{j+1}(\frac{U}{r})\big)(t_{k_{\ell}},r)\to\zeta r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\partial_{r}^{j+2}U,\partial_{r}^{j+1}(\frac{U}{r})\big)(\overline{T}_{*},r)\qquad\text{weakly in $L^{2}$},

\displaystyle\zeta r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\big(\frac{U}{r}\big)_{r}(t_{k_{\ell}},r)\to\zeta r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\big(\frac{U}{r}\big)_{r}(\overline{T}_{*},r)\qquad\text{weakly in $L^{2}$},

and

(10.5)

\displaystyle\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}^{j}U,\partial_{t}^{j}U_{r})(t_{k_{\ell}},r)\to\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}^{j}U,\partial_{t}^{j}U_{r})(\overline{T}_{*},r)

\displaystyle\text{weakly in $L^{2}$},

\displaystyle\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\partial_{r}^{j+2}U(t_{k_{\ell}},r)\to\chi^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\beta}\partial_{r}^{j+2}U(\overline{T}_{*},r)

\displaystyle\text{weakly in $L^{2}$},

\displaystyle(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})(t_{k_{\ell}},r)\to(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})(\overline{T}_{*},r)

\displaystyle\text{weakly* in $L^{\infty}$}.

Here, since
(r,\rho_{0}(r))
only vanish at the boundaries
\{r=0\}
and
\{r=1\}
, respectively, we can obtain the uniqueness of limits in the above by initially applying the weak convergence argument on each interval
[a,1-a]
with
a\in(0,1)
. For example
\eqref{ququ}_{3}
, on one hand,

\zeta r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\big(\frac{U}{r}\big)_{r}(t_{k_{\ell}},r)\to\widehat{F}(\overline{T}_{*},r)\qquad\text{weakly in $L^{2}$ as $\ell\to\infty$},

for some limit
\widehat{F}(\overline{T}_{*},r)\in L^{2}
.
On the other hand, (
10.3
) implies that,
for each
a\in(0,1)
,

U(t_{k_{\ell}},r)\to U(\overline{T}_{*},r)\qquad\text{weakly in $H^{3}(a,1-a)$ as $\ell\to\infty$}.

Thus, for any
\varphi\in C_{\mathrm{c}}^{\infty}(0,1)
, as
\ell\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\Big<\zeta r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\big(\frac{U}{r}\big)_{r}(t_{k_{\ell}}),\varphi\Big>

\displaystyle=\big<U_{r}(t_{k_{\ell}}),\zeta r^{\frac{m-4}{2}}\varphi\big>-\big<U(t_{k_{\ell}}),\zeta r^{\frac{m-6}{2}}\varphi\big>

\displaystyle\to\big<U_{r}(\overline{T}_{*}),\zeta r^{\frac{m-4}{2}}\varphi\big>-\big<U(\overline{T}_{*}),\zeta r^{\frac{m-6}{2}}\varphi\big>=\Big<\zeta r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\big(\frac{U}{r}\big)_{r}(\overline{T}_{*}),\varphi\Big>,

which implies

\widehat{F}(\overline{T}_{*},r)=\zeta r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\big(\frac{U}{r}\big)_{r}(\overline{T}_{*},r)\qquad\text{for {a.e.} $r\in(0,1)$}.

Now, (
10.4
)–(
10.5
), together with (
10.2
)–(
10.3
), Lemma
C.1
, and the lower semi-continuity of the weak convergence, lead to

(10.6)

\mathcal{E}(\overline{T}_{*},U)\leq C(\overline{T}_{*})\mathring{\mathcal{E}}(\overline{T}_{*},U)\leq C(\overline{T}_{*}),\qquad(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})(\overline{T}_{*},r)\in[C^{-1}(\overline{T}_{*}),C(\overline{T}_{*})].

According to Theorem
3.1
and Remark
3.1
, (
10.6
) implies that there exists
T_{0}>0
such that
(U,\eta)
is the classical solution of
IBVP
(
2.2
) on the time interval
[0,\overline{T}_{*}+T_{0}]
, which contradicts to the maximality of
\overline{T}_{*}
. This shows claim (
10.1
).

Therefore, for any
T>0
,
IBVP
(
2.2
) admits a unique solution
(U,\eta)(t,r)
in
[0,T]\times\bar{I}

such that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\big(\mathcal{E}(t,U)+t\mathcal{D}(t,U)\big)+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{D}(s,U)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T),

\displaystyle(\eta_{r},\frac{\eta}{r})(t,r)\in[C^{-1}(T),C(T)]\qquad\text{for all $(t,r)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}$},

where
C(T)\in(1,\infty)
is a constant depending only on
(n,\mu,\gamma,A,\beta,\varepsilon_{0},\rho_{0},u_{0},\mathcal{K}_{1},\mathcal{K}_{2},T)
. It remains to show that
(U,\eta)(t,r)
is actually a classical solution in
[0,T]\times\bar{I}
.

2.
(U,\eta)
is classical satisfying (
2.13
)–(
2.14
).
First, the regularity of
U
and (
2.13
)–(
2.14
) can be proved by the same argument as in Steps 6–7 of §
11.1.2
. Then the regularity of
\eta
follows easily from the formula:
\eta_{t}=U
. Finally, following a similar argument in Step 8 of §
11.1.2
, we can show that
\eqref{eq:VFBP-La-eta}_{1}
holds pointwise in
(0,T]\times\bar{I}
.

This completes the proof of Theorem
2.1
.

11.
Local-In-Time Well-Posedness of the Classical Solutions

In this section, we establish the local well-posedness of
IBVP
(
2.2
) stated in Theorem
3.1
.
Such a local well-posedness theory is highly non-trivial, owing to the strong degeneracy at the vacuum boundary.

In what follows, we denote by
\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}(J)
the space of all functions
f
satisfying
(f,f_{r},\frac{f}{r})\in L^{2}_{\mathrm{w}}(J)
for some interval
J=(0,a)
with
a\in(0,1)
:

\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}(J):=\big\{f:\,\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}(J)}<\infty\big\}\qquad\,\,\text{with}\,\,\,\,\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}(J)}^{2}:=\int_{J}\mathrm{w}\Big(f^{2}+f_{r}^{2}+\frac{mf^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r,

where
\mathrm{w}=\mathrm{w}(r)\geq 0
is a weight function on
J
, and we let
\mathcal{H}^{-1}_{\mathrm{w}}(J):=(\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}(J))^{*}
. In particular, if
J=I
, we simply write
\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}=\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}(I)
.

Before starting the proof, we first give an important property for space
\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}
, which will be used in the subsequent analysis.

Proposition 11.1
.

Let
\mathrm{w}=r^{m}\rho_{0}^{c}
with
c\geq 0
.
Then
\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}
is a reflexive separable Banach space.
Moreover, for any
f\in\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}
, there exists
a sequence
\{f^{\varepsilon}\}_{\varepsilon>0}\subset C^{\infty}(\bar{I})\cap\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{m}}
such that

\|f^{\varepsilon}-f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}}\to 0\qquad\text{as $\varepsilon\to 0$}.

Proof.

The first assertion follows directly from Lemma
A.1
in Appendix
A
. To derive the convergence, we first define
\bm{f}(\bm{y}):=f(r)\frac{\bm{y}}{r}
. Clearly,
\bm{f}
is a spherically symmetric vector function and, due to Lemma
B.1
in Appendix
B
and the fact that
\rho_{0}^{\beta}\sim 1-r
and
\bm{f}\in H^{1}(B_{a})
for any
a\in(0,1)
,
where
B_{a}:=\{\bm{y}:\,|\bm{y}|<a\}
.

Now, we claim that there exists a sequence of spherically symmetric vector functions
\{\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}\}_{\varepsilon>0}\subset C^{\infty}(B_{\frac{3}{4}})
, with the form:
\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}(\bm{y})=f^{\varepsilon}_{\flat}(r)\frac{\bm{y}}{r}
, such that

(11.1)

\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}\to\bm{f}\qquad\text{in $H^{1}(B_{\frac{3}{4}})$ as $\varepsilon\to 0$}.

To obtain this, let
\{\omega_{\epsilon}(\bm{y})\}_{\varepsilon>0}
be the standard spherically symmetric mollifier defined on
\mathbb{R}^{n}
.
Then, due to the standard theory of regularization,
\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}(\bm{y}):=(\bm{f}*\omega_{\epsilon})(\bm{y})
(with
\varepsilon
small) satisfies (
11.1
),
and it suffices to check the spherical symmetry of
\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}(\bm{y})
.
Thanks to Lemma
A.7
, this is equivalent to showing that
\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}(\mathcal{O}\bm{y})=(\mathcal{O}\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon})(\bm{y})
for any matrix
\mathcal{O}\in\mathrm{SO}(n)
. In fact, we have

\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}(\mathcal{O}\bm{y})=\int_{\Omega}\bm{f}(\mathcal{O}\bm{y}-\bm{z})\omega_{\epsilon}(\bm{z})\,\mathrm{d}\bm{z}\qquad\text{for $0<\varepsilon<\frac{1}{100}$ and $\bm{y}\in B_{\frac{3}{4}}$}.

Changing the coordinate
\bm{z}\mapsto\mathcal{O}\bm{z}
, along with
|\mathcal{O}\bm{y}|=|\bm{y}|
and
\det\mathcal{O}=1
, gives

\displaystyle\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}(\mathcal{O}\bm{y})

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}\bm{f}(\mathcal{O}\bm{y}-\mathcal{O}\bm{z})\omega_{\epsilon}(\mathcal{O}\bm{z})(\det\mathcal{O})\,\mathrm{d}\bm{z}=\int_{\Omega}\bm{f}(\mathcal{O}(\bm{y}-\bm{z}))\omega_{\epsilon}(\bm{z})\,\mathrm{d}\bm{z}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}(\mathcal{O}\bm{f})(\bm{y}-\bm{z})\omega_{\epsilon}(\bm{z})\,\mathrm{d}\bm{z}=\Big(\mathcal{O}\big(\int_{\Omega}\bm{f}(\cdot-\bm{z})\omega_{\epsilon}(\bm{z})\,\mathrm{d}\bm{z}\big)\Big)(\bm{y})=(\mathcal{O}\bm{f}_{\flat}^{\varepsilon})(\bm{y}).

This completes the proof of the claim.

Consequently, it follows from (
11.1
) and Lemma
B.1
that

(11.2)

\chi_{\frac{3}{4}}f^{\varepsilon}_{\flat}\in\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{m}},\qquad\,\,\|\zeta f^{\varepsilon}_{\flat}-\zeta f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}}\to 0\qquad\text{as $\varepsilon\to 0$}.

On the other hand, it follows from Lemma
A.1
that there exists a smooth sequence
\{f_{\sharp}^{\varepsilon}\}_{\varepsilon>0}\subset C^{\infty}[\frac{1}{3},1]
such that

(11.3)

\|\zeta^{\sharp}f^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp}-\zeta^{\sharp}f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}}\leq C\|\zeta^{\sharp}f^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp}-\zeta^{\sharp}f\|_{1,\rho_{0}^{c}}\to 0\qquad\text{as $\varepsilon\to 0$}.

Therefore, defining
f^{\varepsilon}:=\zeta f_{\flat}^{\varepsilon}+\zeta^{\sharp}f_{\sharp}^{\varepsilon}
, we see that
f^{\varepsilon}\in C^{\infty}(\bar{I})\cap\mathcal{H}^{1}_{r^{m}}
. Then we can obtain from (
11.2
)–(
11.3
) that

\|f^{\varepsilon}-f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}}\leq\|\zeta f^{\varepsilon}_{\flat}-\zeta f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}}+\|\zeta^{\sharp}f^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp}-\zeta^{\sharp}f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}_{\mathrm{w}}}\to 0\qquad\text{as $\varepsilon\to 0$}.

This completes the proof.
∎

The rest of this section is organized as follows:

§
11.1
:

Establish the global well-posedness of the linearized problem via the Galerkin scheme;

§
11.2
:

Establish the uniform
a priori
estimates for the linearized problem;

§
11.3
:

Establish the local well-posedness of the nonlinear problem via the Picard iteration.

11.1.
Linearization and global well-posedness of the linearized problem

In §
11.1
,
C\in(1,\infty)
denotes a generic constant depending only on
(n,\mu,A,\gamma,\beta,\varepsilon_{0},\rho_{0},u_{0},\mathcal{K}_{1},\mathcal{K}_{2})
, and
C(l_{1},\cdots\!,l_{k})\in(1,\infty)
a generic constant depending on
C

and additional parameters
(l_{1},\cdots\!,l_{k})
, which may be different at each occurrence.

We first linearize problem (
2.2
